**summary:** Athena's reading: the operator's five asks are not blocked by missing features but by a ship door with no deck gate (prebuild carries none; Cloudflare deploys red commits; Verify Live has failed every run since 2026-10-08), a register frozen at r.165 while r.166-r.195 were hand edits (r0.178 unregistered, BUILD.revision 0.166, ledger rows citing parent commits), and a boot QA whose rows were rewritten to agree with the regressions (MARK_SURVIVES_THE_RETURN / MARKS_ARE_REMEMBERED assert remembered marks; the CI's own deck-qa regexes no longer match the deck). Eleven class fixes, each with a red-first test: P0 — put the five node-only deck gates in prebuild and carry/register r0.178 in the same commit; make every ledger release prove its commit declares its revision; close the 'remembered mark' class so no plate is ever red when it stands (true at r0.178 AND r0.195 in QUAL — keepsMark/modeReturns/rangeRelease). P1 — one geometry per plate (drop the r.180 silMesh enlargement, restore the pit wire), TRAIN DOWN's START/ALL DOWN as decide() rows under the light, roundOpen ≡ rangeArmed, five one-stroke pose rasters with words sized by one HUD-card constant and driven by the same Table VI index as MAG n/4, the R-CORE block generated from the shared ledger and gated, and row-body hashing so a QA row cannot be rewritten without a reviewed manifest change. P2 — a deck-gate runner that reports every failure, and Verify Live probing the QR link. Keep af6f65e0 as the functional master (training loop, Table VI, fire gate, true scale, wire, light); keep after it the TRAIN DOWN clock UI, the cold-range end, the pose-card concept and the link handling; drop the enlargement, the loosened roundOpen, the fixed hand-written R-CORE, the filled multi-colour poses, the START bypass and the layout flip-flops. The screenshots do NOT match af6f65e0's CSS (count centred, light on the same row ≈ r0.189's CSS, header unique to af6f65e0) — settle with a headless 428×926 render before pinning the visual master.

### Christo (consensus & user flow)

**verdict_on_current:** The TARGET → APPROVE → FIRE core is intact and identical in both revisions: `designate`, `approveDesig` and `fireN` are byte-for-byte the same at r178 (r178:2047/2427/2515) and r0.195 (cur:2158/2538/2626) — FIRE refuses amber (`AMBER_NO_APPROVE`), a LIVE room needs two separate humans (`TWO_HUMANS`, r178:2440–2443), the approver's seat never fires (`NOT_THE_MARKERS_SEAT`, r178:2520), the HUD line says the next step in words (cur:3041–3044) and `nextStep()` lights exactly one of TARGET/APPROVE/FIRE (cur:980–984, :1004). TRAIN UP is right at both: a hit clears the mark and says "HIT · … · TARGET · APPROVE · FIRE" (r178:2513 `keepsMark` false for `bounce`; cur:2668). What is broken is around that core. (1) QUAL at BOTH revisions keeps the shooter's own red mark through a hit and a lapse (`modeReturns` includes `qual40` r178:2509; `keepsMark` r178:2513; `rangeRelease` short-circuits on it r178:875 / cur:957; the exposure raise at cur:1051 never releases) and `IWQ_VI` re-uses bases (C-150L in phase-1 engagements 3 and 5; C-300 in four phase-2 engagements, cur:886–887), so a plate rises ALREADY RED and fires with no TARGET/APPROVE — r.169 `b6487246` fixed exactly this and `b2dfa80a` wiped it. (2) The amber/red law is not read from the slot phase: `hot()` (cur:2154, r178:2043) is phase-blind, so the silhouette is drawn in `T13.LOCK` red while marked amber (r178:2019) and the reticle goes red on any lock (cur:2022) — shot 2 shows a red plate and red reticle inside an amber box under "AMBER · PRESS APPROVE". (3) At HEAD the fire gate and the light disagree (`roundOpen` cur:2625 loosened by r.193; `startLight`/`rangeArmed` cur:989/:965 unchanged), TRAIN DOWN START is not a decision row (cur:5314 → `downStart()` directly; RESTART goes through `rangeResetOnRecord` → `decide('RESET')` cur:5309–5313), `silMesh` draws a plate at a different size from its amber/red box (cur:1131 vs `slotBoxes` cur:2135 `plateDims` true), and the QR entry path has no reader for `role=` and no route for the `.html`-less link (cur:7703; only `range`/`mode` read at cur:5223–5224; r178 read nothing at all — 0 hits for `enterTheRange`, only `diag=1` at r178:928). The deck's own QA rows were edited to bless the wrong behaviour (`MARK_SURVIVES_THE_RETURN` cur:4648, `qualKept` cur:4664) and the gate that runs them (drone-deck-qa, `EXPECTED_RED=['SYNC_DIRECT']`, tests/deck-qa-manifest.mjs:5) never reaches them because CI dies at traceability step 27.

**fixes:**

1. **A mark lives exactly one exposure of one target, in every mode (QUAL rises already RED)**
   - *class:* Mark lifetime is decided by three mode-keyed predicates (marksPersist/modeReturns/keepsMark) plus a short-circuit in rangeRelease, instead of one declared law: a hit, a lapse or a fall ends the mark; a plate that stands is unmarked.
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/tests/range-2525.test.mjs
     - frontend/tests/deck-qa-manifest.mjs
   - *evidence:* r178:2509 `function modeReturns(){ … return m==='bounce'||m==='qual40'; }` · r178:2513 / cur:2624 `keepsMark` returns true for the shooter's own mark in qual40 · r178:875 / cur:957 `function rangeRelease(q){ … if(keepsMark(q)){ q.mist=false; return; }` · cur:1051 QUAL raise `R.cur=e.bases.map(…q.up=true…mist=false; return q.id; })` with no rangeRelease (r.169 b6487246 added `rangeRelease(q);` there and `||state.rangeMode==='qual40'` to keepsMark; b2dfa80a removed both) · cur:1055 fall path `if(q.fall>=1){ q.up=false; rangeRelease(q); }` is short-circuited · cur:886–887 IWQ_VI repeats C-150L (ph1 eng 3 and 5) and C-300 (ph2 eng 2,3,4,5) · cur:2668 toast 'HIT · … · STILL RED · F WHEN IT STANDS' · approveDesig r178:2432 HOLD 'IT STAYS MARKED · APPROVE WHEN IT STANDS'
   - *why:* The operator's ask 4 is 'in EVERY mode every target needs TARGET → APPROVE → FIRE'. In QUAL today a trainee shoots C-150L in engagement 3, it falls red, and in engagement 5 it stands up already red with FIRE lit — the swarm rehearsal's whole point (everyone targets and approves before firing) is skipped on the most repeated plates of the table.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* frontend/tests/range-2525.test.mjs: lift keepsMark/modeReturns/rangeRelease and the QUAL raise and assert keepsMark(q)===false for qual40 and that the raise calls rangeRelease; rewrite the deck rows MARK_SURVIVES_THE_RETURN (cur:4648) and MARKS_ARE_REMEMBERED (cur:4664 qualKept → qualFresh as r.169 had) and add QUAL_RISES_UNMARKED (expose, mark, approve, hit, let the same base rise in a later engagement → no slot, no desig, FIRE refused NO_RED_BOX); update tests/deck-qa-manifest.mjs.
   - *approach:* Restore r.169's two lines on top of the r178 master (keepsMark excludes qual40; the QUAL raise releases every plate it stands) and delete the 'STILL RED · F WHEN IT STANDS' / 'IT STAYS MARKED' branches since no mode keeps a mark across a fall any more (TRAIN DOWN never returns; TRAIN UP drops on hit since r.166). Enumerate every rangeRelease caller (fall, lapse, raise, lane change, reset) and make the one law hold at each.
2. **The deck's own QA rows assert the OLD flow and the gate that runs them never runs**
   - *class:* QA rows edited to match the code instead of the ask, hidden behind an earlier red step; EXPECTED_RED exempts only SYNC_DIRECT so deck-qa is red by design at r178 and HEAD.
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/tests/deck-qa-manifest.mjs
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
     - frontend/tests/traceability.test.mjs
     - docs/traceability/drone-2525.ledger.json
   - *evidence:* cur:4648 `push('MARK_SURVIVES_THE_RETURN', hit1&&red1&&held&&back&&hit2, 'one mark, one approval: … back up still red …')` contradicts cur:2624 keepsMark=false for bounce (r.166) · cur:4664 `const qualKept=!qq.up&&!!markSlotOf(qq.id)` blesses the QUAL gap · tests/deck-qa-manifest.mjs:4 lists both rows, :5 `EXPECTED_RED = ['SYNC_DIRECT']` · scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs:3–7 'every failing row is in EXPECTED_RED' · r.195 14a930d0 rewrote the layout row to `on[0].left<on[2].left<on[1].left` · CI dies at traceability (rev 61 `commit: PENDING`) before deck-qa (plan file §B)
   - *why:* A gate that cannot reach the journey rows cannot defend the journey; the rows now document the wrong behaviour as correct, so any future 'fix' that restores the ask will turn them red and invite another edit of the row instead of the code.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* Unblock traceability (fill rev 61's commit, file rev 60's shipped) so test:ci reaches drone-deck-qa; rewrite the three rows to the ask (TRAIN UP hit → mark gone → TARGET lit; TRAIN DOWN marks batch-approved then fired; QUAL rise unmarked); keep EXPECTED_RED at SYNC_DIRECT only; add a deck-qa-manifest assertion that no row id was removed between r178's manifest and HEAD's without a ledger row naming it.
   - *approach:* Treat the row text as the operator's words, not the code's: each row's sentence must quote the ask (docs/asks/2026.10.11_18.56..53…md) and the row must fail on af6f65e0 where af6f65e0 is wrong (QUAL) — prove the gate bites before shipping the fix.
3. **Amber and red are not read from the slot phase — the silhouette and the reticle are red while the box is amber**
   - *class:* One phase, three painters: the voxel box and the caption read sl.phase, but hot() and the reticle's 'near' rule are phase-blind, so RED appears on the glass before APPROVE.
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
   - *evidence:* cur:2154 / r178:2043 `function hot(o){return !!(state.desig&&o&&(o.id===state.desig.id||o===state.desig.ref));}` (no phase) · r178:2019 `(mine?own:segs)(silMesh(q),(hot(q)||q.mist)?T13.LOCK:…)` draws the marked plate red · cur:2022 `const col=near?T13.LOCK:T13.SI; /* r.143: a white bullseye that turns red when the pip is on a target */` · correct painters: cur:2135 slotBoxes `col:red?T13.LOCK:T13.GIMBAL` and cur:2080 caption `ph=…phase==='red'?'RED':'AMBER'` · shot 2 (docs/asks/img/…shot2.png): red silhouette + red reticle inside the amber box, HUD 'AMBER · T2 · 200 M RIGHT · PRESS APPROVE'
   - *why:* The operator's law is literal — 'targets turn Amber when targeted and after approved turn red' — and the swarm rehearsal depends on a second person reading the colour at a glance: today the plate itself says RED (fire) while the strip says AMBER (wait). The r.133 comment 'LOCK is aim, never authority — it is not drawn in the red box's colour' (r178:1951) already states the principle the plate and reticle violate.
   - *priority:* P1
   - *gate:* New deck QA row PHASE_COLOUR_FOLLOWS_THE_SLOT: mark → every stroke list for that plate (silMesh colour, slotBoxes col, caption ph, reticle col) is GIMBAL; approve → all LOCK; nothing marked → SI. Add a source assertion in range-2525.test.mjs that hot() takes the phase (or that the plate colour expression reads sl.phase).
   - *approach:* Replace hot(o) with phaseOf(o) → 'red'|'amber'|null read from state.tgtSlot (one reader the painter, the caption, the reticle and the QA row share, like plateCaptionRects is for captions); the reticle turns amber on a marked target, red only on a red one, white otherwise.
4. **Two predicates for one question — may this seat fire? — the light says WAIT while FIRE works**
   - *class:* roundOpen (the gate) and rangeArmed (the light, the lapse clock) diverged in r.193; the trainee reads one clock and is judged by another.
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/tests/range-2525.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* cur:2625 `function roundOpen(){ … if(ph==='PRACTICE'||ph==='LIVE'||!!state.qaArmed) return true; const intro=…; return !!(intro&&intro.style.display==='none'&&ph!=='LIVE'); } /* r.193 */` · cur:965 `function rangeArmed(){ … return ph==='PRACTICE'||ph==='LIVE'||!!state.qaArmed; }` · cur:989 startLight `if(!rangeArmed()) return state.rangeMode==='qual40'?'O':'R';` · cur:1058 lapse counted only `if(…rangeArmed()&&lapseIsMine()&&!q._eng)` · r178:2514 roundOpen === rangeArmed · r.192 cur:5185 already sets PRACTICE on enterTheRange, making r.193 redundant for the QR path
   - *why:* Every mode's journey starts at the light (WAIT · READY · FIRE, START_LIGHT_WORDS cur:4723). If FIRE is accepted under a red WAIT lamp the words on the glass are false, and a QUAL lapse under that state is not scored — the record and the picture disagree.
   - *priority:* P1
   - *gate:* Extend the deck row NO_FIRE_BEFORE_THE_ROUND with LIGHT_AND_GATE_AGREE: for each phase (WAITING with intro hidden, PRACTICE, LIVE, OVER) assert (startLight()!=='R' && !=='O') === roundOpen() on CH0; range-2525.test.mjs lifts roundOpen and rangeArmed and asserts they are the same function body.
   - *approach:* Delete the r.193 clause (keep r.192's PRACTICE-on-entry); make roundOpen() = rangeArmed() = one function `rangeArmed`, and have startLight, nextStep, designate, approveDesig, fireN and rangeLapse all call it.
5. **TRAIN DOWN's START and its time are not on the record and never reach the other phone**
   - *class:* A state change that is not a decision row: START mutates plates and a local stopwatch directly while RESTART/RESET/RELOAD/HOLD/REJECT all go through decide() and linkSend.
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/tests/drone-decisions.test.mjs
     - frontend/scripts/drone-team-e2e.mjs
   - *evidence:* cur:5314 `_rr.onclick=()=>{ if(chNum()===0&&state.rangeMode==='stay'&&state.downGo!=='run'&&…){ downStart(); toast('START · HIT EVERY TARGET IN THE LANE'); … return; } if(rangeResetOnRecord('BUTTON')) toast('RESTARTED · '+modeSentence()); }` · cur:954 downStart() raises plates and zeroes downT with no decide() · cur:5309–5313 rangeResetOnRecord → `decide('RESET','RANGE',{why,mode,…})` · cur:1044 `if(state.rangeAllDown){ state.downGo='done'; toast('ALL DOWN · '+downClockWord()); }` — the time is a toast, not a row · cur:2488–2496 decide() is the only writer that linkSends HOLD/REJECT/RESET/RELOAD · operator addendum 8: the TRAIN DOWN time is the seed of the 3v3 scoring
   - *why:* In the room the host presses START, the other seat's plates stay down and its clock never runs; the 'ALL DOWN · m:ss.t' the operator wants to compare across teams exists only as a toast on one phone. R-CORE cannot replay who started and when.
   - *priority:* P1
   - *gate:* New deck QA row TRAIN_DOWN_START_IS_A_ROW: START → a decide('START','RANGE',{mode:'stay',n:<targets>}) row; the last plate down → decide('ALL_DOWN','RANGE',{t:…}) row carrying the elapsed time; the two-phone e2e (drone-team-e2e) asserts the joiner's plates stand after the host's START and both records hash the same rows.
   - *approach:* Route START through the same path as RESET (rangeResetOnRecord('START') or a sibling that calls decide and linkSend), apply the row on peers like RESET is applied at r178:2169, and compute the time from the two rows' t, not from a display counter. Keep aac4db9b's light words (START / TIME / ALL DOWN, cur:1010).
6. **The TRAIN DOWN target set is undeclared — the operator says 'all 3', the deck raises all 11**
   - *class:* A mode's scoring set exists only as an implementation side-effect (platesHere) rather than a declared, recorded number.

   - *why:* The time only means something if every team shoots the same set; 'how fast to target all 3' versus 'all 11' is the difference between the demo he described and a different drill.

7. **The QR / ?range= entry path has no contract: role is never read, the link has no route, and it can never reach the room where consensus lives**

   - *why:* The share sheet is how a second trainee is meant to join the lane; today it either does nothing (r178), lands on the SPA (HEAD if Workers does not strip .html for this asset), or lands in solo practice where one person is both humans (fAppr → approveDesig('HI-2'), cur:5169). The swarm rehearsal — everyone targets and approves — is unreachable from the QR.

   - *gate:* New frontend/tests/drone-range-entry.test.mjs: lift shareUrl() and the URLSearchParams reader from the carried deck and assert every param the sheet writes (range, role, mode) is read and applied, that mode words round-trip (train-up/train-down/qual40 ↔ RANGE_MODE_NAME), and that the link target is a real asset (play.html) or a listed redirect; verify-live.yml: curl the exact URL the sheet emits and require `id="playHud"` and `revision:'0.<REV>'` (not the SPA).
   - *approach:* Make the link `play.html?…` again (the asset that exists), define role as a seat in the lane's room (turret = marker, the other seat = approver) and have the reader either join/offer the room for that lane or say on the glass that it is practice until a second phone joins; one mode-word map shared by the sheet and the reader.

   - *class:* One target, two sizes — silMesh scales any plate under 28 px while plateRect/plateHit/slotBoxes/drawTbox keep plateDims true, so the amber/red box can be smaller than the plate a trainee is looking at and a hit on the drawn plate misses.

   - *why:* Amber/red legibility is the box around the plate; if the box and the plate disagree the colour cue is unreadable and FIRE on a visibly boxed plate can be a MISS. The r.138/r.141 true-scale rule was the reason 300 m plates read as a person at 1× — zoom is the tool, not a scale hack.
   - *priority:* P0

   - *approach:* Revert silMesh to r178:1026–1031 and redraw RANGE_WIRE; if a 300 m plate must stay legible on a phone, do it in the ONE place that already owns the angular floor (pipFloorPx, 3 mrad) or by the zoom cue — never by a second size.
9. **The pose card: five filled multi-colour SVGs in a box of its own size, not one-stroke rasters the same height as the magazine cards and the light**
   - *class:* A HUD card drawn outside the vector law (fill, per-position colour) and outside the row's shared height, driven by the right index but the wrong picture.

**keep_from_r178:** - designate / approveDesig / fireN as the one journey (r178:2047, :2427, :2515 — byte-identical at cur:2158/:2538/:2626): amber on TARGET, red on APPROVE, FIRE refuses amber (AMBER_NO_APPROVE) and nothing (NO_RED_BOX)
- The LIVE-room consensus rules: two separate humans (approveDesig r178:2440–2443, reason TWO_HUMANS), the approver's seat never fires (fireN r178:2520, NOT_THE_MARKERS_SEAT), approve BY SLOT (r178:2428), a HOLD/REJECT is one row on every peer (decide r178:2377)
- TRAIN UP: a hit clears the mark and the glass says 'HIT · <plate> · TARGET · APPROVE · FIRE' (keepsMark false for bounce r178:2513; fireN r178:2554) — exactly shot 1
- TRAIN DOWN batch consensus: mark several, approve several, fire each red one; a hit is for good (modeReturns excludes stay r178:2509; MARKS_ARE_REMEMBERED TRAIN DOWN part)
- The words on the glass: hudPhase 'RED · Tn · <plate> · PRESS FIRE' / 'AMBER · Tn · <plate> · PRESS APPROVE' / 'LAST · …' / 'TARGET FIRST' (cur:3041–3044 = r178); designate's one amber sentence with '· NOW PRESS APPROVE' or '· WAIT FOR THE OTHER SEAT TO APPROVE' (r178:2065); nextStep lighting exactly one of TARGET/APPROVE/FIRE (r178:898); captions only while marked, phase-coloured (plateCaptionRects)
- roundOpen === rangeArmed === PRACTICE|LIVE|qaArmed (r178:883, :2514) and the start light WAIT · READY · FIRE read from the same clock (r178:906)
- True-scale plates (silMesh r178:1026–1031 = plateDims), the voxel box amber/red around every marked target (slotBoxes r178:2023–2025), the firing-pit RANGE_WIRE drawn after the plates
- QUAL table: IWQ_VI order, 40 targets, four magazines of ten, magReload refuses a fifth (r178:839), one round per target per exposure, lapse = UNFIRED MISS on the host clock
- The magazine coloured by rounds left (r0.178 itself, af6f65e0) and the HUD vocabulary 'MAG n · k RDS · ZOOM', 'TRAIN UP · HIT · MISS'
- RESET as a decision row applied on every peer with the mode and the targets (r178:2169, rangeResetOnRecord) — the pattern START must copy

**r178_screenshot_match:** No — af6f65e0's CSS does not produce the operator's two screenshots. af6f65e0 r178:29 `#magWell{position:absolute;left:8px;top:92px;…gap:12px}` with r178:31–33 `width:86px … min-height:112px` puts RELOAD and the count card side by side at the left under the status line, and r178:50 `#startLight{position:absolute;right:8px;top:42px}` puts the light ABOVE that row; no JS moves them (0 hits for enterTheRange/toRange at r178). Shots 1–2 (1284×2778 ≈ 428 css px wide) show RELOAD at the left, the count card CENTRED (x ≈ 460/924) and the stop light on the SAME row at the right, cards roughly 56×70 css px — the layout of a146c120 (r0.189, 'Put the round count back in the center'), not of af6f65e0. Everything else in the shots IS af6f65e0's behaviour: header 'eXeL r0.178', 'LAST · 150 M LEFT · DOWN', the magazine drawn gold with '3' in red (r.178's colour-by-count), 'MAG 1 · 3 RDS · ZOOM 4.8×/30×', 'TRAIN UP · HIT 27 · MISS 0', 'HIT · 150 M LEFT · TARGET · APPROVE · FIRE' with TARGET lit (r.166 keepsMark=false for bounce), and in shot 2 'AMBER · T2 · 200 M RIGHT · PRESS APPROVE' with APPROVE lit, an amber voxel box, 'LOCK / 200 M RIGHT' — and the red silhouette + red reticle inside the amber box that fix 3 names. So: af6f65e0's LOGIC is the master; its HUD-row CSS is not what the operator saw (deploy.yml:5 records hand `wrangler deploy`s as the only Deployment-History source). A headless render of af6f65e0 at 428×926 is required before any byte of it is called the visual master; until then the two screenshots are the visual master for the HUD row.

**minor_notes:** - play.html's T13 palette (amber GIMBAL #F0A020, red LOCK #E24B3B, cur:728 = r178:650) is not the 13 trinity hexes of lib/trinity-palette.ts; the deck predates the law and its amber/red are what the operator has learned to read — the pose rasters should use ONE of the deck's own strokes (SI or WIRE) so the card matches the plates on the same glass, and the 13-law gate should scope to the raster sources, not repaint the deck
- In PRACTICE one person is both humans: #fAppr → approveDesig('HI-2') (cur:5169); the words 'NOW PRESS APPROVE' are honest about that. The real swarm rehearsal needs the room — lobbyCanLaunch (cur:3347–3353) allows only 1v1, DIRECT, codex-authenticated, full rosters, so 'everyone' today means two phones; a gate should state that scope so nobody reads the QR as a six-phone join
- enterTheRange (cur:5185) silently turns a WAITING room into PRACTICE if the phase is not LIVE — a joiner who scans the QR while the host is in the waiting room drops out of the room without a word
- A MISS keeps the box red for the rest of the exposure (fireN cur:2668 'r.131: red holds for THIS exposure'); that is per-TARGET approval, consistent with 'every target must have Target, Approve, Fire' — leave it, say it in the QA row
- range-2525.test.mjs:91 is red at HEAD only because the words changed (fix 11); :92 is red because RANGE_WIRE is gone (fix 8) — both go green on the r178 master
- hudPhase's idle word 'TARGET FIRST' (cur:3044) is correct and is what shot8 shows; shot8's bunched plates are fix 8, not a words bug
- The fixed R-CORE badge (bottom 8–50 px) does not overlap TARGET/APPROVE/FIRE (#face sits at bottom 148 px in turret mode, cur:152) — it breaks the shared rule, not the finger path
- The deck's self-QA has no row for the TRAIN DOWN clock, the pose card, the QR reader or 'QUAL rises unmarked' — four of the five asks are ungated today
- Sensor Fusion (app/SensorFusion-2525) is untouched by every fix here and keeps its own ledger.ts; it works and was built with the Grok bot — credit it in the record
- Every fix lands as a new appended revision (r.196+) with a ledger row citing its own commit, a carried snapshot and a HASHES file, and DECK_REV/deck-rev.ts/verify-live pinned to it — never by editing r.178's or r.195's bytes in place

**summary:** Through the consensus-and-flow lens, r0.178 and r0.195 share an intact TARGET → APPROVE → FIRE core (designate/approveDesig/fireN are byte-identical, with the LIVE two-humans and marker-fires rules) and a correct TRAIN UP; what breaks the operator's five asks sits around that core. P0: QUAL at both revisions keeps a shooter's red mark through a hit/lapse so a repeated plate rises already RED and fires with no TARGET/APPROVE (r.169 fixed it, b2dfa80a wiped it); the deck's own QA rows bless that and the old TRAIN UP, and the gate that runs them never runs; and HEAD draws plates at one size while boxing and hitting them at another (the distorted-targets class). P1: amber/red is not read from the slot phase (hot() and the reticle are phase-blind — shot 2 shows a red plate inside an amber box), the fire gate and the light diverged in r.193, TRAIN DOWN's START and time are not decision rows and never reach the other phone, its target set is undeclared (he said 3, the deck raises 11), the QR/?range= path reads no role, routes nowhere at HEAD and did nothing at r178, and can never reach the room where consensus lives; the pose card is filled multi-colour art at its own height; the range's R-CORE words are a hand-written fork. Keep af6f65e0's logic as master, keep from after it the TRAIN DOWN clock (re-wired through decide), the cold-range end, qualDropStrays, the pose driver and the ?range= reader; drop the silMesh floor, r.193's clause, RCORE_HIST/fixed badge, the filled stances and the edited QA rows. af6f65e0's CSS does not produce the operator's screenshots (count centred, light on the same row) — render it headless before calling its bytes the visual master. Every fix named above has a gate: extend range-2525/deck-qa rows or add drone-range-entry.test.mjs, and CI must reach drone-deck-qa again.

### Enki (diversity & edge cases)

**verdict_on_current:** r0.195 fails the operator's ask at the edges where a demo gets tried: at 1× the far plates are drawn 4–5× their hit outline (cur:1131 inside silMesh scales any plate under 28 px; plateRect cur:1069–1074, reticleNear cur:2181 and pipOn in fireN cur:2649 keep true size), at the QUAL 3× cap the same plate is ~1.5× its box and at 30× it is exact — so "targets don't work" depends on the zoom; a QUAL plate that stands again (C-300 is up in engagements 5, 7, 8, 9, 10; C-50 in 1, 11, 15 — cur:886–889) comes back already RED because keepsMark/modeReturns (cur:2620/2624) keep the shooter's own mark across the fall and the raise at cur:1051 never releases a slot — the same at r0.178 (r178:2509/2513), and the deck's own row asserts the gap (cur:4664 qualKept); roundOpen (cur:2625) opens the fire gate on any hidden intro while rangeLapse (cur:1059), startLight (cur:989) and qualTimer (cur:998) still key on rangeArmed, so a line can fire and never lapse; TRAIN DOWN's START (cur:954/5314) and ALL DOWN (cur:1042) are not decide() rows, so a second phone in the room never sees the clock, a RESTART mid-run leaves no time, and the armed state says ALL DOWN · PRESS RESTART (cur:1041→2183) beside a button that says START (cur:1008/2584); the QR writes role=turret (cur:7703) that nothing reads (cur:5223–5224) and an unknown mode silently becomes TRAIN UP (cur:5176); the pose card is five filled, five-coloured SVGs (cur:43–54, 453–457) whose words live in a second table (cur:1013 vs cur:886–889) and whose magazine number is a reload counter (cur:905–906), so a partial reload in phase 1 shows MAG 3/4 under PRONE SUPPORTED and leaves phase 4 with NO MAG; R-CORE is a 5-row fork under a position:fixed badge over the dock (cur:68/161/709–715). r0.178 (af6f65e0) is the right LOGIC master — true-scale silMesh (r178:1026), the strict roundOpen (r178:2514), TRAIN UP's re-arm after a hit (r178:2513), the magazine and Table VI rules — but it already carries the QUAL mark gap, has no TRAIN DOWN clock, shows positions only as a toast, and its CSS (r178:29–37, :50) does not produce the operator's screenshots.

**fixes:**

1. **One screen-size law for a plate: drawn outline = hit outline = amber/red box, at 1×, 3× and 30×**
   - *class:* Three consumers of a plate's projected size with two laws (true scale vs a 28 px floor) — the 'distorted targets' class
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/tests/range-2525.test.mjs
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
   - *evidence:* cur:1131 `if(px>0&&px<28){ const s=28/px; D={w:D.w*s,h:D.h*s}; }` (silMesh, every range mode, viewMode!=='map'); cur:1069–1074 plateRect uses plateDims(q) unscaled; cur:2181 reticleNear and cur:2649 pipOn (fireN) read plateRect; r178:1026–1029 silMesh `const D=plateDims(q);` with no scale; cur:937 focal 1827.6 px at 1× → a 300 m E (1.016 m, range-2525.test.mjs:45) ≈ 6 px → ×4.5 at 1×, ≈ 18 px → ×1.5 at the QUAL 3× cap (cur:940), ×1 at 30×; plan file §C shot8 'silhouettes bunched and overlapping at the left edge'
   - *why:* The amber/red box and the hit test sit on a plate a third the size of what is drawn, and the error changes with the optic: a shooter who learns the picture at 1× misses at 3×; at 1× eleven enlarged plates overlap each other and the lane boards (the operator's shot8). r.141's life-size rule and the r.138 zoom law were the invariant.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* range-2525.test.mjs: source assertion that silMesh, plateRect and drawTbox share one dims function and no `28/px` survives; drone-deck-qa row PLATE_DRAWN_EQUALS_HIT_BOX at 1×/3×/30× on the 50 R and the 300 (drawn outline height == plateRect height ±1 px, box width == drawn width) in portrait and landscape
   - *approach:* Restore r178's silMesh (true scale) and keep legibility through the one declared floor already in the code (pipFloorPx cur:942, an angle, zoom-invariant) applied in ONE function `plateScreen(q)` that silMesh, plateRect, reticleNear and drawTbox all read; never a second floor.
2. **A mark lives for one exposure in every mode: a plate that stands again needs TARGET → APPROVE → FIRE (QUAL included), a lapsed plate releases its box**
   - *class:* Authority scoped to the exposure (r.131) broken by a mark that outlives its target in QUAL and TRAIN modes
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
     - frontend/tests/range-2525.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* r178:2509 and cur:2620 `modeReturns … return m==='bounce'||m==='qual40'`; r178:2513 / cur:2624 keepsMark returns true for the shooter's own mark in qual40; cur:957 rangeRelease `if(keepsMark(q)){ q.mist=false; return; }` (so the lapse at cur:1059 and the fall at cur:1057 keep the slot); cur:1051 raises the next engagement setting `q.mist=false` only — tgtSlot untouched; cur:2633 HOLD TARGET_RETURNING 'STILL RED · F WHEN IT STANDS'; cur:4664 `qualKept=!qq.up&&!!markSlotOf(qq.id)` asserted by MARKS_ARE_REMEMBERED cur:4666; cur:886–889 C-300 stands in engagements 5,7,8,9,10 and C-50 in 1,11,15; b6487246 (r.169) had the fix, b2dfa80a wiped it; cur:2619 marksPersist is already false in a LIVE room (rooms never had the gap)
   - *why:* The operator's rule is per target per mode: amber on TARGET, red on APPROVE, then FIRE. In QUAL a plate hit (or lapsed) in engagement 5 comes back in engagement 7 already red and fires on F — the swarm rehearsal he describes (everyone targets and approves before each shot) is exactly what is skipped, and the deck's own QA row certifies the skip.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* drone-deck-qa rows QUAL_MARK_DIES_WITH_THE_EXPOSURE (hit C-300 in eng 5, run to eng 7: no slot, fireN → REJECT NO_RED_BOX, TARGET lit), QUAL_LAPSED_MARK_RELEASED, TRAIN_UP_REARMS_AFTER_HIT (the r.166 behaviour, kept); rewrite MARKS_ARE_REMEMBERED's QUAL half and MARK_SURVIVES_THE_RETURN (cur:4648–4654 still asserts the r.162 'F,F,F'); range-2525.test.mjs source assertion that modeReturns/keepsMark exclude qual40 and that the engagement raise at the rangeTick calls rangeRelease on every raised plate
   - *approach:* keepsMark(q) → false for bounce AND qual40 (solo and room), rangeRelease unconditional on fall/lapse, and the engagement raise releases any slot on a raised plate; keep TRAIN DOWN's 'a hit is for good' and the multi-mark board (T1…Tn) as is.
3. **One armed predicate: nothing fires where nothing lapses and the light is dark (roundOpen ≡ rangeArmed)**
   - *class:* Two predicates for 'the line is hot' — the fire gate and the lapse/light clock diverged in r.193
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/tests/range-2525.test.mjs
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
   - *evidence:* cur:2625 roundOpen `return !!(intro&&intro.style.display==='none'&&ph!=='LIVE')` vs r178:2514 `return ph==='PRACTICE'||ph==='LIVE'||!!state.qaArmed`; cur:965 rangeArmed unchanged; cur:1059 rangeLapse only `rangeArmed()&&lapseIsMine()`; cur:989 startLight returns 'O' (dark) in QUAL when not armed; cur:998 qualTimer ''; cur:5288 beginReh hides the intro with no PRACTICE; cur:5185 enterTheRange already sets PRACTICE, so r.193's loosening buys nothing on the range path
   - *why:* A tab that reached the picture through REHEARSAL/BEGIN or any future door fires with the light dark, the timer blank and no lapse ever booked: a 40/40 QUAL with zero unfired misses is possible, and two phones (one PRACTICE, one not) book different tables from the same shots.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* range-2525.test.mjs source assertion that roundOpen and rangeArmed are one body (or roundOpen calls rangeArmed); drone-deck-qa row NOT_ARMED_NEVER_FIRES_OR_LAPSES (intro hidden, lobby SETUP: designate refused, fireN → REJECT ROUND_NOT_OPEN, 0 LAPSE rows over a full engagement, light O)
   - *approach:* Restore r178's roundOpen; keep enterTheRange's PRACTICE (r.192) as the one way the range arms a solo line; every entry path that hides the intro either arms the line on the record or leaves TARGET refusing with the r.135 sentence.
4. **TRAIN DOWN's START and ALL DOWN are rows that travel: one clock per lane on every phone, a time that survives RESTART and a locked phone**
   - *class:* A mode state kept outside decide() — not on the record, not on the peer, not replayable
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
     - frontend/tests/drone-decisions.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* cur:954 downStart sets downGo/downT with no decide(); cur:5314 the button calls it directly; cur:1042 'done' → `toast('ALL DOWN · '+downClockWord())` only; cur:2494 decide links only HOLD/REJECT/RESET/RELOAD; cur:2280 a peer RESET → resetRangePlates(); goRange() → cur:5379 downArm() (the joiner's plates stay down while the host's clock runs; its own START starts a second clock); cur:1017–1020 laneRestartFromRow raises `up=train` on a TRAIN DOWN lane regardless of arming; cur:5309–5313 the RESET row carries qualR/qualH but no downT; cur:895 rangeDt caps a hidden tab at 0.25 s per tick so the clock is engine time, not wall time (two phones disagree after a lock screen)
   - *why:* The operator's addendum 8 makes this clock the seed of the 3v3 challenge ('best time'); a time that exists only in a toast on one phone cannot be compared, replayed or credited, and a room shows two different START states for one lane.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* drone-deck-qa rows TRAIN_DOWN_START_IS_A_ROW (one decide START row with lane, mode, wall t0), TRAIN_DOWN_TIME_IS_A_ROW (a DOWN row with the elapsed time = t_down − t0 from the rows, not state.downT), PEER_START_RUNS_MY_CLOCK (applyWorld of a peer START row on my lane → downGo 'run'), RESTART_MID_RUN_RECORDS_THE_ABANDONED_TIME; drone-decisions.test.mjs: the verb list includes START/DOWN
   - *approach:* START = decide('START','RANGE',{lane,mode:'stay',t0:wall}) that linkSends like RESET; ALL DOWN = decide('DOWN','RANGE',{t}) written by the lane's clock owner (lapseIsMine rule); the displayed time is derived from the two rows; the peer handler runs downStart on the same lane; rangeResetOnRecord carries downT when a run is abandoned.

10. **Every mode switch, RESTART and lane change resets every mode's state on one row, and asks before discarding a running TRAIN DOWN clock as it asks for a live QUAL table**
   - *class:* Per-mode state reset piecemeal by whichever path noticed it

   - *approach:* One `rangeZero(nextMode)` that enumerates the whole class (mag, marks, qual tower, down clock, RANGE_RUN, lastShot, toasts) called by rangeReset, seatLane and the peer RESET handler; the guard treats a running clock like a live table.

   - *approach:* One release = one carried snapshot + patch + HASHES + register row + ledger row + pin bump, in one commit, as r.165 and earlier did; never a hand edit of play.html on main.
12. **TRAIN DOWN raises a declared number of targets (the operator's 'all 3'), carried on the START row, not every plate in the lane**
   - *class:* A mode parameter hard-wired to the lane's inventory
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
   - *evidence:* cur:954 downStart raises `platesHere()`; cur:1188 `42 lanes × 11 silhouettes`; cur:4477 TRAIN_ALL_UP asserts 11; ask verbatim 'timer under stop light tonsee how fast to target all 3'; addendum 8 'challenges targeting number of targets'; at 1× the 200–300 m plates are 6–9 px (cur:937) so eleven at once are not plainly targetable on a phone
   - *why:* Eleven plates at 1× is where the bunching shows and where the demo stops being 'low complexity'; the future 3v3 challenge scores 'a number of targets', so the number must be a parameter on the record from the start. Needs the operator's word on 3 vs all.
   - *priority:* P2
   - *gate:* drone-deck-qa row TRAIN_DOWN_RAISES_N (the START row's n plates stand, nearest first; ALL DOWN after exactly n hits); TRAIN_ALL_UP kept for TRAIN UP
   - *approach:* START row carries `targets:N` (default 3 if the operator confirms); downStart raises the first N of a declared order (50 R, 50 L, 100 C …); the strip says 'N UP'.
13. **A page reload mid-table leaves a row (or the sheet says a reload ends the table)**
   - *class:* State that dies with the tab without a word on the record
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
   - *evidence:* cur:812–813 localStorage 'exel-2525-sets', cur:1551 'exel-2525-soak', cur:3953 VOICE_KEY, cur:4888 'exel-2525-perf' — no range/mode/magazine/table key; cur:5224 the URL re-enters only lane+mode; the QR (cur:7703) carries the same
   - *why:* A phone that reloads with 7 rounds and 23/40 shot comes back on TRAIN UP, MAG 1 · 30 RDS, with no RESET row — the other phone in the room still shows the old table.
   - *priority:* P2
   - *gate:* drone-deck-qa row PAGEHIDE_WRITES_A_ROW (pagehide → one RESET row with why:'PAGE', the table's qualR/qualH or downT) or, if the operator chooses not to persist, the sheet's sentence gated by the i18n test
   - *approach:* On pagehide write the RESET row through decide() (it already travels); do not persist the table — a reload is a reset, said once.

**keep_from_r178:** - True-scale silhouettes: silMesh with no size floor other than the angular pip floor (r178:1026–1029, pipFloorPx r178 'PIP_FLOOR_MRAD=3') — the r.138/r.141 law the screenshots show (the 150 m plate under the reticle at 4.8× is drawn at its real size)
- The strict fire gate: roundOpen = PRACTICE | LIVE | qaArmed (r178:2514), rangeArmed (r178:883), designate refusing a down plate (r178:2049), approveDesig red, fireN refusing AMBER_NO_APPROVE / NO_RED_BOX (r178:2515ff)
- TRAIN UP re-arm after a hit: keepsMark false in bounce (r178:2513) and the 'HIT · … · TARGET · APPROVE · FIRE' sentence the operator's shot1 shows; RETURN_S=3 (r178:818)
- The Table VI program and its clock: IWQ_VI/IWQ_ENG (r178:807–813), EXPOSURE_BY_COUNT 5/8/12/16, ENG_GAP_S 3, PHASE_GAP_S 10, engagementAt, rangeTick's exposure loop (r178:934–966), rangeLapse as an UNFIRED MISS row (r178:885), one round per target per exposure
- The magazine law: magCap 10×4 in QUAL / 30 elsewhere (r178:823), magReload refused when full and the fifth refused on the record (r178:839), RELOAD calls at the phase rest, the round colours green >20 / amber 6–20 / red ≤5 (af6f65e0's own change) that shot1 shows as the red '3'

- rangeReset as the one reset (r178:873), rangeResetOnRecord with the 'press again to discard N rounds' guard (r178:5176–5181), a RESET row that reaches every peer (r178:2169), seatLane refusing a lane change during a live QUAL
- The status words on the glass: 'LAST · 150 M LEFT · DOWN', 'AMBER · T2 · 200 M RIGHT · PRESS APPROVE', 'LOCK 200 M RIGHT', 'TRAIN UP · HIT 27 · MISS 0' — all present in shot1/shot2 and in af6f65e0
- The mode names and the mode/lane/RESTART row (RANGE_MODE_NAME r178:864, the three options r178:381–386)

**keep_after_r178:** - The TRAIN DOWN clock inside the stop light (aac4db9b r.182: #startWord START/TIME/ALL DOWN + #startTime, downClockWord, the strip's clock at cur:2584) — kept only once START and ALL DOWN are decide() rows (fix 4)
- QUAL ends cold (50826908/84c86cf9 r.179/181): qualFinish lowers every plate and clears marks (cur:971), #qualOver CEASE FIRE / UNLOAD / SHOW CLEAR, RELOAD → UNLOAD, qualDropStrays (cur:1021) — a phantom never stands
- The phasegap raise that lowers last exposure's plates beside the new ones (cur:1052, r.180) and the 'CEASE FIRE · LOCK AND LOAD · <position>' tower word (cur:1061) — with the position word read from the engagement (fix 6)
- The one-pose-at-a-time card concept and qualStance's index logic (cur:1012: STANDING only while engagement 1 is up, the next pose during the phasegap, RANGE IS COLD at the end) — keep the logic, replace the art and the words' source
- #app.qual class toggle (cur:1003) and the compact card row at the magazine line's height (r.186 6a4097f6 / r.189 a146c120's intent: RELOAD left, count beside, light right — the screenshot's geometry)
- enterTheRange + the ?range=&mode= door and the QR sheet (bb7bb3cd r.167, 63a135a0 r.174, ddb5108d/43a0dc46 r.187/192) — kept under one link contract (fix 8); r.192's PRACTICE arming at cur:5185
- BUILD.revision brought back in step with the corner (44631941) — and from now the register chain (fix 11)
- Sensor Fusion (app/SensorFusion-2525): untouched; it works and was built with the Grok bot — credit it in the record

**drop_after_r178:** - The 28 px silhouette floor in silMesh (2960980f r.180, cur:1131) — the 'distorted targets' class; and any second size law that is not the one plateRect/pipOn read
- The r.193 roundOpen (074ac55c, cur:2625) — a hidden intro is not an armed line
- The hand-written R-CORE: RCORE_HIST, the position:fixed #rcoreBadge, the full-screen #rcoreHist sheet, the two-tap badge, the 'approved' kind (14a930d0 r.195, cur:68–77, 709–727, 5316) and the already-deleted REV_BOOK pane (3ba0f97c r.190)
- The filled, five-colour inline SVG poses (3380e7b1 r.194 / 8206a28b, cur:43–54, 453–457) and the five-colour palette off the T13 set
- The deck QA rows edited to certify broken behaviour: qualKept in MARKS_ARE_REMEMBERED (cur:4664–4666), the r.195 layout-order row ('order=…on[0].left<on[2].left<on[1].left'), and MARK_SURVIVES_THE_RETURN's r.162 'F,F,F' assertion (cur:4648–4654) that contradicts the r.166 TRAIN UP rule
- The silent fall-through of an unknown ?mode= to TRAIN UP (cur:5176) and the unread role= parameter (cur:5223–5224 vs 7703)

- The 'LOCK r.195 … The r0.178 magazine row does not move' comment on BUILD (cur:730) and every 'Release/Lock' commit that re-edited play.html on main without a carried snapshot, hash, patch, register row and ledger row; the reused numbers r.187–r.189 get correction rows, not re-edits
- The 'PRESS RELOAD · MOVE' removal is not restored as a toast — the reload call and the move cue are the RELOAD card's glow (reloadCalls) and the pose card (fix 6/7), one word each

**r178_screenshot_match:** No — by measurement, not by text. shot1/shot2 are 1284 px wide = 428 CSS px × 3. The RELOAD card is ≈ 56 × 70 CSS px at x ≈ 8; the count card is at x ≈ 195 (centre-left, not at 8+86+12 = 106); the light is ≈ 42 px wide on the SAME row (top ≈ 44 CSS px under the two bars) and the 'TRAIN UP · HIT 27 · MISS 0' strip sits UNDER the cards (≈ 130). af6f65e0's CSS gives r178:29 `#magWell{left:8px;top:92px;…gap:12px}`, r178:31 `width:86px`, r178:32–33 `min-height:112px` (two 86×112 cards side by side at top 92) and r178:50 `#startLight{top:42px}` (the light ABOVE the cards). The card:light width ratio is ≈ 1.33 in the shots and ≈ 2.9 in the CSS, so it is not phone zoom. What DOES match af6f65e0: the corner 'eXeL r0.178', 'LAST · 150 M LEFT · DOWN' (r178:2932), 'MAG 1 · 3 RDS · ZOOM 4.8×/30×' on the magBar line, the red '3' (af6f65e0's round colours), 'HIT · 150 M LEFT · TARGET · APPROVE · FIRE' after a hit (keepsMark false in bounce, r178:2513), the amber 3D box and red reticle, true-scale plates. The ledger's own later rows describe the SHOT, not the commit: row 463 (r0.186) 'Both cards stay compact, 52 by 72 … The r0.178 picture is the master' and row 484 (r0.189) 'RELOAD is the left card. The round count is the center card. The light is the right card.' Conclusion: the live r0.178 the operator photographed was not the committed af6f65e0 bytes (a hand wrangler deploy or an uncommitted working copy — deploy.yml notes hand deploys); treat af6f65e0 as the LOGIC master and the screenshots as the PICTURE master, and prove the picture with a headless render at 428×926 compared to the shots' card geometry (x, width, height, row) — not with af6f65e0's CSS.

**minor_notes:** - #board (cur:104, top 74 left 8) overlaps the magazine cards (top 62) but is empty on the range (cur:2690) — harmless until a scene writes it; fold into the one-height card row.
- zoomMax snaps 15× → 3× when switching to QUAL (cur:5327) but leaves 3× when switching back to training; acceptable, state it in the mode sentence.
- The ALT-C 25 m sheet (onSheet) runs every mode: the pose/engagement/magazine mapping and the N-target TRAIN DOWN must hold on the sheet too (C-50L → C-50 at cur:947); add a sheet variant to POSE_MATCHES_MAG and TRAIN_DOWN_RAISES_N.
- In a LIVE room marksPersist is already false (cur:2619), so rooms never had the QUAL mark gap and approveWorksHere needs a second human — the fix to keepsMark must not change the room path; gate both solo and LIVE.
- hudMag shows 'MAG n/4' only in QUAL and an unbounded 'MAG 7' in training (cur:909–910) — fine; the 30-round training magazine and 'reload any time' stay the operator's rule.
- rangeDt caps a hidden tab at 0.25 s per tick (cur:895): a locked phone in TRAIN DOWN under-counts its clock versus a phone left on — the START/DOWN rows with wall timestamps (fix 4) make the recorded time the same on both.
- The firing-pit wire: RANGE_WIRE is still drawn at HEAD under chNum()===0 (cur:1913) — the drone-playable gate fails on its ORDER relative to drawLaneLines (plan §B), not on its absence; keep the gate's order rule when restoring the draw order.
- Verify Live reached the deck check on af6f65e (plan §B) so the site served SOMETHING labelled r0.178 that day; the sha256 step would have told us whether it was the commit's bytes — one more reason the register chain (fix 11) is not bookkeeping.
- drone-deck-qa.mjs VIEWPORTS (:26) never renders 320 px or 428×926; every layout claim in this review above 390 px is by CSS arithmetic until that gate runs.

**summary:** Through the edge-case lens r0.195 breaks at exactly the seams a demo gets tried on: the zoom (plates drawn up to 4.5× their hit box at 1×, exact at 30×), the second exposure of the same plate in QUAL (already red, fires without TARGET/APPROVE — the same gap at r0.178), the unarmed line (fires, never lapses), the second phone (TRAIN DOWN's START/ALL DOWN are not rows), the mid-table switch (half-reset state, a START button beside an 'ALL DOWN · PRESS RESTART' toast), the QR link (role unread, unknown mode silently TRAIN UP, two solo lines), and the pose card (filled five-colour SVGs, words in a second table, the magazine number a reload counter that drifts from the phase). Five P0 classes: one plate-size law for draw/hit/box; a mark that lives for one exposure in every mode; roundOpen ≡ rangeArmed; TRAIN DOWN START/DOWN on the record and on every phone; R-CORE from the shared ledger in page flow. P1: engagement as the one source for pose/words/magazine with the QUAL reload rule; a pose raster per the vector law at one card height gated at 320/390/428 and landscape; one link contract; one armed sentence; one whole-class reset; the register chain r.166–r.195 restored so a phone can say which bytes it runs. Keep af6f65e0 as the logic master (true scale, strict gate, TRAIN UP re-arm, Table VI, magazines, light), keep the TRAIN DOWN clock, the cold QUAL end, the pose-card logic and the range door from after it, drop the 28 px floor, the loosened roundOpen, the forked R-CORE, the filled poses and the QA rows edited to certify the breakage. The operator's r0.178 screenshots do not match af6f65e0's CSS (cards 56×70 on the light's row vs 86×112 under it) — they are the picture master, to be proven by a headless render, not by the commit. Sensor Fusion is untouched and credited.

### Enlil (implementation & build verification)

**verdict_on_current:** r0.195 (HEAD play.html, 547,290 B, sha256 7b02aed7…) is a 35-commit chain of direct hand edits of the served file since the last registered deck r.165 (`git log 47f66e21..HEAD -- play.html` = 35 commits, all author alexseguin-hbs; `git log 47f66e21..HEAD -- docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/` = only 4bb4a3f7, the r.165 ship). Nothing after r.165 is carried, hashed, patched or registered: README HEAD, REVISIONS.md (38 rows, chain intact, last r.165), `tests/deck-head.mjs:5 DECK_REV='165'`, `lib/drone-2525/deck-rev.ts:3`, `HASHES_r165`, `patches/r164_to_r165.py` and the domain JSON (project.revision 0.050, last release names r.165, shipped PENDING) all still say r.165, so the three register gates are red exactly as the explorers said and as I re-ran read-only: deck-consistency 47/4, drone-playable 111/4 (served ≠ carried; no `revision:'0.165'`; "PRESS RELOAD · MOVE" 1→0; `RANGE_WIRE.forEach` gone from the range branch cur:1989), traceability 60/1 (rev 61 `commit: PENDING`). r0.178 (af6f65e0, 531,981 B, sha256 d39875be…) is not a carried file either — it exists only as `play.html` at that commit, its header says r0.178 (r178:318) while `BUILD.revision` says '0.166' (r178:652), and it still lacks the r.169 QUAL fix (`modeReturns` r178:2509 includes qual40; `keepsMark` r178:2513 excludes only bounce; `rangeRelease` r178:875 keeps a kept mark) because b2dfa80a wiped r.167–r.176. Its bytes do carry what the operator calls working: true-scale plates (no `px<28` enlarge), the firing-pit wire drawn (r178:1886), the r.135 `roundOpen` (r178:2514), the r.165 reselect/ring/mark logic, the 48×96 magazine drawings with round colours, the QR sheet. The ledger (72 entries) was never edited in place (rows 1–60 byte-equal to 4bb4a3f7) but what was appended is wrong: rev 60 shipped PENDING and rev 61 commit PENDING can never go green under the current gates (PENDING allowed only on the LAST release; `git cat-file -e PENDING` fails), revs 64–72 cite the parent commit (I verified 3380e7b serves r0.194 not r.195, 074ac55 r0.193, 43a0dc4 r0.192, 7ffd164 r0.187, 61b4d49 header r0.189/BUILD 0.186), there is no row for r0.178, r.187–189 were used twice, and `drone-ledger.gen.ts` was hand-copied (the generator emits only rev/date/kind/text/commit — no `shipped` — yet the gen.ts carries 20 `shipped` keys, and no gate holds the Drone gen to its source). The sandbox has no `frontend/node_modules`, but playwright 1.56.1 (/opt/node-tools) and chromium-1194 (/opt/pw-browsers, the first candidate path in deck-qa/team-e2e/range-lanes) are present, so after `npm ci` the whole headless chain can run locally before any push.

**fixes:**

1. **Carry the base before patching it: r0.178 and r0.195 become carried, hashed, registered files; r.166–r.195 are recorded as unregistered hand edits (append, never edit)**
   - *class:* unregistered revision — bytes served that no register row, hash or patch names
   - *files:* - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/REVISIONS.md
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/README.md
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/drone-2525_r.178.html (new, = af6f65e0 bytes)
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/drone-2525_r.195.html (new, = 14a930d0 bytes)
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/HASHES_r178.sha256
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/HASHES_r195.sha256
     - frontend/tests/drone-revisions.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* `git log 47f66e21..HEAD -- frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html` = 35 commits (d6bcc160 … 14a930d0); `git log 47f66e21..HEAD -- docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/` = only 4bb4a3f7; REVISIONS.md last row `| r.165 | 2026-10-05 | Claude Code | 465812 | 76961252… |`; af6f65e0 play.html sha256 d39875bec3f4…ba30e8f66, 531,981 B, header r178:318 `<span>r0.178</span>`, r178:652 `revision:'0.166'`; HEAD sha256 7b02aed7…, 547,290 B, cur:349 r0.195, cur:730 `revision:'0.195'`; drone-revisions.test.mjs:16-22 (every carried file ≥ r.128 needs a row whose sha/bytes match; every row needs a carried file), :23 (strictly increasing), :25 (chain = sha256(prev|rev|sha|bytes)), :26 (a `## r.NNN — ` section per row); register header: 'skipped numbers are never invented'
   - *why:* The patcher pattern (`patches/rNNN_to_rNNN.py`, SRC = carried file) has no SRC to start from: r.178 exists only inside git. A patch that reads `git show af6f65e0:…` instead of a carried file would be unreproducible from the directory the gates read (deckUrl → docs/…/drone-2525_r.NNN.html; range-2525/sequencer-2525/lobby-2525 lift functions from the CARRIED file). Carrying both r0.178 (the operator's baseline) and r0.195 (what the site served when he reported) with rows that say 'carried from commit X by the operator account, hand edit, not built by a patch here; header/BUILD disagree' makes the gap a fact on the record and keeps the chain hash honest. r.187/188/189 were each used twice (ddb5108d/040a21ba/61b4d49d then 7ffd164e), so those numbers cannot be reused: the next number is r.196.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* frontend/tests/drone-revisions.test.mjs (existing chain/sha/bytes/section checks turn green on the two new rows) + NEW frontend/tests/deck-register-coverage.test.mjs: every commit on main that changed public/drone-2525/play.html since the last register row is named in the register (a row, or the 'unregistered hand edits' section with commit, header stamp, BUILD stamp, sha256, bytes) — fails on any unnamed play.html commit
   - *approach:* (1) `git show af6f65e0:frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html > drone-2525_r.178.html` and the same for 14a930d0 → r.195; sha256sum both into HASHES_r178/HASHES_r195. (2) Append two table rows (author 'operator account (Grok), hand edit — carried from commit af6f65e0 / 14a930d0', bytes, sha, 'shipped in' = the commit itself since the file IS the served bytes) with chain hashes computed by the test's formula. (3) One narrative section '## r.178 — carried from af6f65e0' and '## r.195 — carried from 14a930d0' plus a sub-list 'unregistered: r.166 d6bcc160 … r.194 3380e7b1' giving per commit the header string, BUILD.revision, sha256 and bytes, and the three facts: BUILD stayed 0.166 r.177–r.185; r.187–189 used twice; b2dfa80a wiped r.167–r.176. (4) README HEAD moves only when r.196 ships. Never touch an existing row (drone-revisions:25 breaks every later chain).
2. **The patch declares and asserts its base; the gate reads the base from the register, not from N−1; CI re-runs the patch and proves the carried bytes**
   - *class:* asserted patcher provenance — a patch whose source is inferred, not proven
   - *files:* - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/patches/r178_to_r196.py (new)
     - frontend/tests/deck-consistency.test.mjs
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/HASHES_r196.sha256
     - frontend/tests/drone-playable.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* deck-consistency.test.mjs:32-34 `const prev = String(+DECK_REV - 1); const patchPath = …/patches/r${prev}_to_r${DECK_REV}.py; ok(fs.existsSync(patchPath)…)` and :36-37 (HASHES_r{REV} must carry the patch sha); patches/r164_to_r165.py: `SRC=…drone-2525_r.164.html; DST=…r.165.html`, `rep(old,new,count)` refuses on count mismatch, final `REFUSE: stale` on `revision:'0.164'`/`r0.164`, prints `bytes … sha …` — but never asserts SRC's sha256; r178 longest line 416 = 2,352 chars (the inlined QR lib, r178:5277-5281 `var qrcode = function()`), so line-number patches are unsafe and exact-count replacements are the only honest form
   - *why:* With base r.178 and next number r.196 the current gate demands a file named `r195_to_r196.py` — a lie (the base is r.178, not the served r.195). Generalising the gate to read the base from the register section ('Patch: `patches/r178_to_r196.py` from r.178') and to verify the patch's declared BASE_SHA against the carried base file fixes the class: a patch can never silently run against the wrong bytes (the r.165 patch would happily run on any r.164-looking file). Re-running the patch in CI and comparing the output sha to the register row makes 'built by asserted patch' a proven claim rather than a sentence.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* frontend/tests/deck-consistency.test.mjs extended: parse `Patch: \`patches/r(\d+)_to_r${DECK_REV}.py\`` from the DECK_REV section; require the file; require a `BASE_SHA=` line equal to sha256 of drone-2525_r.<base>.html; run `python3 -I <patch> --check` (the patch writes DST to a temp path when --check is given) and assert the produced sha equals the register row sha for r.196 and the served public/drone-2525/play.html sha (drone-playable:18 already holds served == carried)
   - *approach:* Write r178_to_r196.py in the r164_to_r165.py shape: header comment = the operator's verbatim ask + addenda numbers; `BASE_REV='178'; BASE_SHA='d39875be…'`; refuse if sha256(SRC) != BASE_SHA; every edit via `rep(old,new,count)` with exact counts; final refuse on `r0.178`, `revision:'0.166'`, `revision:'0.178'`; stamp header AND BUILD to 0.196 in the same step; print bytes+sha. Record the patch sha in HASHES_r196 beside the html sha. Steps inside the patch are the fixes the other lenses name (QUAL r.169 re-apply, TRAIN DOWN clock on record, pose card, R-CORE block, layout), each with its own QA row appended to the deck's boot QA.
3. **Green gate chain locally, then ONE revision commit, then STOP until Verify Live is green on that SHA; put the deck gates at the door that actually ships**
   - *class:* outpacing the deploy — pushes that ship with the gate red
   - *files:* - .github/workflows/deploy.yml
     - .github/workflows/verify-live.yml
     - frontend/package.json (prebuild, test:ci)
     - frontend/scripts/ship.sh
     - frontend/scripts/status.mjs
   - *evidence:* deploy.yml `gate` job runs `npm run test:ci` (red since d6bcc16 per explorer B) and the `ship` job is `if: needs.gate.outputs.canbuild == 'true'` (skipped without the two Supabase secrets — 'Cloudflare's git build is the only deployer of this sha'); package.json `prebuild` = lockcheck, integrity-guard, gen-seal-html, copy-*, gen-i18n-index, sign-invariant, sign-lexicon-voice, sensor-fusion-r4a, financial-surface — NO deck gate, so Cloudflare's build ships a play.html the repo's own gates reject; verify-live.yml:113-119 reads DECK_REV from deck-head.mjs by sed, greps `revision:'0.$REV'` and compares the live sha256 to the REVISIONS.md row; CLAUDE.md:4-12 'ONE revision at a time … STOP … The bookkeeping commit waits too'; test:ci order: traceability (step ~27) runs long before drone-playable/range/revisions/deck-consistency and deck-qa/range-lanes/team-e2e are last, so one PENDING hides every deck gate
   - *why:* Every push since 2026-10-08 shipped through Cloudflare with the gate red and Verify Live red; the register, the pins and the site diverged because nothing at the shipping door read the deck gates. The fix is procedural AND structural: the executor's chain must be green before push (the sandbox can run it: Node 22, Python 3.11, playwright 1.56.1 at /opt/node-tools, chromium-1194 at /opt/pw-browsers = the scripts' first candidate — only `npm ci` is missing), and Cloudflare's prebuild must fail closed on the two cheapest deck gates so a hand edit of play.html can never ship again.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* package.json `prebuild` += `node tests/drone-playable.test.mjs` (needs the ts-alias loader → use `node --experimental-strip-types --loader ./tests/ts-alias-loader.mjs`) and `node tests/deck-consistency.test.mjs` (both read-only, ~1 s; traceability needs full git history so it stays in test:ci); verify-live.yml unchanged (already the proof); `npm run status` line in every report

**r178_screenshot_match:** No. af6f65e0's CSS cannot produce shot1/shot2. Measured on shot1 (1284 px wide = 428 CSS px at 3×, the deck's own EYE device): RELOAD card ≈56×70 CSS px at the left, the count card centred under 'MAG 1 · 3 RDS · ZOOM 4.8×/30×', the stop light at the right on the SAME row and top as the cards, the HUD status line above them. af6f65e0's only rules are r178:29 `#magWell{position:absolute;left:8px;top:92px;gap:12px}`, r178:31 `width:86px`, r178:32-33 `min-height:112px`, r178:34 `svg{width:36px;height:82px}`, r178:50 `#startLight{right:8px;top:42px}` and the single media rule r178:182 `#startLight{top:42px}` — two 86×112 cards side by side at the left with the light 50 px ABOVE them; no JS positions #magWell. Zoom cannot explain a centred count card (it would stay adjacent to RELOAD). I traced every commit r0.179–r0.191: 50826908/2960980f/84c86cf9/aac4db9b/04d76634 keep the 86 px/left-8/top-92/light-42 CSS; the only centred layout is a146c120 (r0.189: `#magWell{left:0;right:0;top:78px}`, 70 px cards, light top:78 — same row) and its header reads r0.189, not r0.178. So the picture he calls 'r0.178' was served by bytes no commit holds (deploy.yml's own AAR notes hand `wrangler deploy`s), or by a working tree between commits. Status text matches r0.178 ('LAST · 150 M LEFT · DOWN', MAG/RDS/ZOOM line, round colours, header string), so the FUNCTIONAL baseline is af6f65e0; the VISUAL master (RELOAD left · count centred · light right · one row · ~56×70 cards with the 48:96 drawing) is nearest a146c120. Settle it as proposed: headless-render af6f65e0 and a146c120 at 428×926 beside shot1 before writing the CSS block, then gate the layout by measurement.

**summary:** Through the build-verification lens the problem is provenance before it is behaviour: 35 hand edits of the served play.html since r.165 with zero register touches, pins frozen at '165', a patcher pattern with no carried base, a ledger whose appended rows are wrong and whose gates cannot accept a correction, a generated file hand-copied, and a shipping door (Cloudflare prebuild) that never reads a deck gate — so red CI shipped for three days. The honest next revision is r.196, built by `patches/r178_to_r196.py` from a newly CARRIED af6f65e0 (with r0.195 carried too, and r.166–r.195 recorded as unregistered hand edits by appended sections, never edits), with the patch declaring and asserting its base sha and the deck-consistency gate reading the base from the register instead of N−1 and re-running the patch to prove the bytes. r0.178's bytes are the functional master (true-scale plates, pit wire, strict roundOpen, r.165 mark logic, 48:96 magazines) but they lack the r.169 QUAL fix and their CSS does not render the operator's screenshots — the visual master must be chosen by a headless render of af6f65e0 vs a146c120 at 428×926 and then gated by measurement (one card height, centred count, 0.5 svg aspect, rounded-square pose). Keep from after r0.178 the TRAIN DOWN clock (routed through decide()), the cold-range end, the pose slot/mapping/words, the shared-link reader and THE RANGE intro; drop the <28 px enlargement, the pit-wire removal, the r.193 roundOpen loosening, the fixed in-file R-CORE fork, the filled pose SVGs, the edited QA rows, the reused numbers and the hand-copied gen.ts. Gate chain before push (after `npm ci`; playwright 1.56.1 and chromium-1194 are already on the box): build:parity → traceability → drone-playable → range/sequencer/lobby on the NEW carried file → drone-revisions → deck-consistency → rcore-revisions → drone-deck-qa (both viewports, manifest appended with the six new rows) → range-lanes → team-e2e → test:ci; one commit; push; STOP until Verify Live is green on that SHA; then the bookkeeping commit. Add drone-playable + deck-consistency to Cloudflare's prebuild so a hand edit can never ship again.

### Krishna (integration & cross-module)

**verdict_on_current:** Through the integration lens the defect is not in play.html alone; it is that every seam around play.html has been cut. (1) The door that ships is not the door that gates: Cloudflare Workers Builds deploys each push from `npm run build`, whose `prebuild` (frontend/package.json:117) runs no deck gate, while deploy.yml's `gate` (test:ci) is red on every main push since d6bcc160 and `ship` is skipped — on 8f9b3540 the Deploy run 38190050790 failed at "Test (headless suites …)" with ship skipped, yet Verify Live run 38190050780 reports "Poll live site for 8f9b354" = success and fails only at "Verify the served operator deck". The site serves bytes no gate passed. (2) The deck's R-CORE is a fork: cur:709–715 `RCORE_HIST` (five hand rows, kind `approved` outside traceability.test.mjs:11's KINDS), cur:68 `#rcoreBadge{position:fixed…z-index:9}` (the shared rule rcore-revisions.test.mjs:107 forbids fixed), cur:70 a full-screen `#rcoreHist` sheet, cur:697 a hard-coded CLOSE — while the React route for the same surface mounts `<RCoreBadge history={fromLedgerJson(DRONE_LEDGER)}>` (command-ux1.tsx:36–38,47,147) with 72 ledger rows. r0.178 has no history at all (r178:644 `rcoreStep` + r178:510 `#rcoreLoop` only). (3) Every pin says r.165 while the bytes say r0.195: deck-head.mjs:5, deck-rev.ts:3 → the PLAY button label (command-ux1.tsx:99) lies; verify-live.yml:113–118 reads DECK_REV + the REVISIONS.md sha and goes red; REVISIONS.md:48 last row r.165 with shipped "PENDING (Verify Live)"; domain JSON project.revision 0.050 / last release r.165 shipped PENDING; ledger rev 60 shipped PENDING, rev 61 commit PENDING, revs 65–72 cite the parent commit (rev 72 "r.195" cites 3380e7b = "Draw the five QUAL stances", r.194's code); drone-ledger.gen.ts carries 20 `shipped` keys the generator (gen-rcore-revisions.mjs:58–64) never emits (header line 1 says DO NOT EDIT BY HAND) — so it is hand-copied, and `LedgerEntryInput.shipped` (revisions.ts:58) was added to the shared type only to make it compile. (4) The share-link contract is unowned: r178:7568 emits `/drone-2525/play.html?range=&role=&mode=` but r178 reads only `?diag=1` (r178:928; no URLSearchParams anywhere) — the QR does nothing at r0.178; cur:7703 emits the extensionless `/drone-2525/play?…` (served only because Workers Static Assets strips `.html`, with `not_found_handling: single-page-application` wrangler.jsonc:10–18 turning any miss into a 200 SPA shell), cur:5223–5224 reads `range` and `mode`, `role` is never read, and no test or script references `shareUrl`/`range=` (grep hits only drone-intro.test.mjs:22, the React intro) — the r.167 handler was wiped by b2dfa80a with nothing going red. r0.178 is the right code base for the range itself, but as an integration unit it is unregistered, unpinned, link-dead and history-less.

**fixes:**

1. **The door that ships must run the deck gates (Cloudflare prebuild) — or stop being a door**
   - *class:* ship door bypasses the gate (deployer ≠ gater)
   - *files:* - frontend/package.json
     - frontend/scripts/build-gate.mjs
     - .github/workflows/deploy.yml
     - .github/workflows/verify-live.yml
     - frontend/tests/ship-door.test.mjs (new)
   - *evidence:* frontend/package.json:117 `prebuild` = lockcheck · integrity-guard · gen-seal-html · copy-pdf-worker · copy-migration · gen-i18n-index · sign-invariant · sign-lexicon-voice · sensor-fusion-r4a · financial-surface — no drone-playable / deck-consistency / traceability / drone-revisions; deploy.yml:96 "Cloudflare's git build is the door that actually ships"; deploy.yml:98–101 ship SKIPPED without the Supabase secrets; build-gate.mjs:3–9 "it is OUT of prebuild until that log is read"; Actions API: Deploy 38190050790 gate step "Test (headless suites …)" = failure, ship = skipped; Verify Live 38190050780 "Poll live site for 8f9b354" = success, "Verify the served operator deck" = failure; next.config.js:12 WORKERS_CI_COMMIT_SHA (Workers Builds is the builder)
   - *why:* CLAUDE.md:4–10 (never outpace the deploy; every invariant gets a gate) is structurally void while the only deployer runs none of the deck invariants: 30 hand edits of play.html shipped through a red gate. The restore to r0.178 will ship the same way unless the door changes first — and Grok's test role depends on the live site being a gated artefact.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* NEW frontend/tests/ship-door.test.mjs (source-level, modelled on tests/worker-api-routes.test.mjs): package.json `prebuild` contains a `test:deck` group = drone-playable + deck-consistency + drone-revisions + traceability + rcore-revisions (all pure Node, no Chromium, seconds); deploy.yml names that group as its own step. Proof on the site: verify-live.yml's existing deck step goes green on the first push after the fix.
   - *approach:* Add `test:deck` to package.json and run it from `prebuild` (Node-only gates, so the Cloudflare builder fails on a red deck gate and the edge stays on the last green build) — the class fix is "the deployer runs the invariants", not another GitHub-side check. Alternatively set the four secrets so deploy.yml's `ship` is the only deployer and disable the Cloudflare git build; either way the ship-door gate asserts the chosen configuration. Keep build-gate.mjs's Node-version guard pattern (loud skip, never silent).
2. **The deck's R-CORE history must be the shared ledger, rendered the shared way — not a hand list**
   - *class:* forked record (two histories for one surface)
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/scripts/gen-rcore-revisions.mjs
     - frontend/lib/2525-core/revisions.ts
     - frontend/tests/rcore-revisions.test.mjs
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
   - *evidence:* cur:709–715 `var RCORE_HIST=[{rev:'0.178',kind:'approved',…}…]` (5 rows; `approved` ∉ KINDS at traceability.test.mjs:11); cur:68 `#rcoreBadge{position:fixed;…z-index:9}` vs rcore-revisions.test.mjs:107 "never a fixed overlay"; cur:70 `#rcoreHist{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:70}`; cur:697 `<button id="rcoreClose">CLOSE</button>`; cur:724 renders `r.rev · r.kind` + text only (no date, commit, title/detail, compare); command-ux1.tsx:36–38,47 `fromLedgerJson(DRONE_LEDGER)` and :147 `<RCoreBadge history=…>` = 72 rows on /main/Drone-2525 and /drone-2525/; ledger rev 72 text "R-CORE now matches Vision-2525" (false); r178:644–650 only `rcoreStep` and r178:510 `#rcoreLoop` — r0.178 has no history; ledger rows 19 and 22 name a model (redacted only by revisions.ts:21–24 at render, which the deck never calls)
   - *why:* The operator's ask 5 verbatim: "R-Core history must match other modes". Today the same surface shows 72 rows on the React route and 5 invented rows on the deck; a static HTML deck cannot import RCoreBadge, so parity has to come from ONE generated source the deck reads, or it will fork again on the next hand edit.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* EXTEND frontend/tests/rcore-revisions.test.mjs §7: (a) play.html contains no `RCORE_HIST`, no `kind:'approved'`, no `#rcoreBadge{position:fixed`; (b) `public/drone-2525/rcore-ledger.json` exists, entries.length === ledger entries.length, last rev equal, every kind ∈ KINDS, no MODEL regex hit (pre-redacted); (c) the deck fetches that path and mounts the badge in flow (`data-rcore-badge`, mounted last). Runtime: a deck-qa row `RCORE_IS_THE_LEDGER` (list length === JSON entries, two clicks icon→wordmark→list) — runs headless in drone-deck-qa and on the LIVE deck via verify-live.yml's "Boot QA on the LIVE deck".
   - *approach:* Teach gen-rcore-revisions.mjs to emit, beside drone-ledger.gen.ts, a static `public/drone-2525/rcore-ledger.json` from the SAME ledger (rev, date, kind, title=firstSentence, detail, commit; redactModelIds applied at generation because the deck has no adapter). The deck's badge becomes the shared two-click pattern (icon → wordmark → list) in page flow, mounted last, reading that JSON; list rows carry rev · date · kind · title · commit chip like rcore-revision-panel. play.html's bytes then never change when the ledger appends (the hash register stays stable). Drop `shipped?` from LedgerEntryInput unless the generator emits it.
3. **One release = one atomic register set; the served bytes, the pins, the ledger and the generated module must name the same edition**
   - *class:* register drift (served ≠ registered; hand-copied generated file; PENDING never filled; parent-commit citations)
   - *files:* - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/REVISIONS.md
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/README.md
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/HASHES_r*.sha256
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/patches/
     - docs/drone-2525/drone-2525.v00.00.json
     - docs/traceability/drone-2525.ledger.json
     - frontend/lib/2525-core/drone-ledger.gen.ts
     - frontend/tests/deck-head.mjs
     - frontend/lib/drone-2525/deck-rev.ts
     - frontend/tests/deck-consistency.test.mjs
     - frontend/tests/rcore-revisions.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* deck-head.mjs:5 `DECK_REV='165'`, deck-rev.ts:3 `"165"`, command-ux1.tsx:99 label `r.`+DECK_REV → "r.165" while cur:730 `revision:'0.195'`; REVISIONS.md:48 last row r.165, shipped column "PENDING (Verify Live)"; last carried file drone-2525_r.165.html, last patch r164_to_r165.py; domain JSON project.revision 0.050, last release shipped PENDING; ledger rev 60 shipped PENDING, rev 61 commit PENDING, rev 72 commit 3380e7b (= "Draw the five QUAL stances", the r.194 code; r.195 is 14a930d0), rev 71 commit 074ac55 (= r.193 code); no ledger row for r.167–175, r.177–185 (so none for r0.178 = af6f65e0); drone-ledger.gen.ts:1 "GENERATED … DO NOT EDIT BY HAND" yet carries 20 `"shipped"` keys the generator (gen-rcore-revisions.mjs:58–64) never emits; revisions.ts:58 `shipped?: string` added by 61b4d49d "unblock the build"; r178:652 `revision:'0.166'` under a header saying r0.178; deck-consistency.test.mjs:32–34 demands `r${DECK_REV-1}_to_r${DECK_REV}.py`; verify-live.yml:113–118 reads DECK_REV and the register sha
   - *why:* Three gates (drone-playable, deck-consistency, verify-live) already encode the invariant and are red; the restore cannot go green by editing pins alone because r.166–r.195 were never carried, the patch convention assumes N-1 exists, and the ledger cites commits that serve other revisions. Appending honest rows is the only path that keeps the chain and the law (a new edition is an append, never an edit).
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* EXISTING frontend/tests/deck-consistency.test.mjs + drone-revisions.test.mjs + drone-playable.test.mjs + traceability.test.mjs; EXTEND deck-consistency: the patch's from-rev is read from HASHES_rNNN ("from r.178") and must be a carried row, instead of assuming N-1; EXTEND rcore-revisions.test.mjs: `node scripts/gen-rcore-revisions.mjs --check` (new flag) — every *.gen.ts byte-equals the generator's output; EXTEND traceability.test.mjs: a release's `commit` must touch play.html with the BUILD.revision the row names (git show <sha>:play.html | grep revision:'0.NNN').
   - *approach:* Append (never edit): carry af6f65e0 as `drone-2525_r.178.html` and 14a930d0 as `drone-2525_r.195.html` with register rows marked "hand edit, served unregistered" (chain intact), then r.196 = the restore with `patches/r178_to_r196.py` and HASHES_r196 naming its base; bump DECK_REV in both pins in the same commit; BUILD.revision, the header and the corner equal 0.196; ledger: one `correction` row stating revs 65–72's true commits and the missing r.167–185 editions, then the r.196 `release` row (commit = artefact sha; `shipped` filled by the bookkeeping commit only after Verify Live is green, CLAUDE.md:9–10); regenerate drone-ledger.gen.ts with the script (emit `shipped` from the generator if the type keeps it).
4. **The share link (QR / ?range=) is one contract, named once, asserted at the deck, the React PLAY link and the live site**
   - *class:* unowned cross-module URL contract (producer and consumer drift; soft-404 masks a miss)
