**minor_notes:** - worker.js:139–150 inspects the PATH only (query strings never inspected), so `?range=21&mode=train-up` can never be WAF-blocked; `run_worker_first` then falls through to env.ASSETS.fetch, where Static Assets' html_handling serves play.html for the extensionless form — Cloudflare's 307 on `.html → clean` is the same hop worker.js:19–22 warns drops a #fragment (not a query), so the query survives; still, probe it live rather than assume.
- drone-deck-qa.mjs:17 serves PUB by exact path (no .html stripping) — any local test of the share link must use the play.html form; a generated public/drone-2525/rcore-ledger.json is served by the same server, so the deck's ledger fetch works headless, in Verify Live's LIVE run and on the site.
- The ledger's `route` is /main/Drone-2525 (ledger-note) while the deck serves at /drone-2525/play.html; one ledger for both surfaces is correct — the deck's rows are already the 'Operator deck r.NNN served at /drone-2525/play.html' releases — so parity is a rendering problem, not a second ledger.
- Sensor Fusion keeps its own ledger.ts and worker.js:171–175 sets its Permissions-Policy; nothing here touches it — credit the Grok bot for it in the record as the operator asked.
- REVISIONS.md:48's shipped column still says 'PENDING (Verify Live)' for r.165 and ledger rev 60 shipped PENDING: the bookkeeping commit the law (CLAUDE.md:9–10) requires was never made for r.165 either — the r.196 bookkeeping commit should close both.
- deploy.yml:98–101 'ship SKIPPED' because the Supabase secrets are not set as Actions secrets — the GitHub door has never shipped; the AAR at deploy.yml:3–9 described exactly today's failure mode (the site serving a build no gate passed) in July.
- The lexicon default for drone.play_deck still reads 'PLAY · r.128' (lexicon-data.ts:3413); harmless at runtime because of the replace, but it is a third place that names a revision.

**summary:** Integration verdict: the range's code base should be af6f65e0 (r0.178) plus a short list of carried-forward pieces (the ?range= reader, the r.169 QUAL gate, the TRAIN DOWN timer on the record, the cold-range end, the pose-card logic), but no restore is safe until three seams are fixed as classes: (P0) the Cloudflare build — the only thing that actually deploys — runs none of the deck gates, so a red gate has shipped 30 hand edits and will ship the restore the same way; put a Node-only `test:deck` group in `prebuild` (or make deploy.yml's ship the only door) and gate that configuration. (P0) The deck's R-CORE is a five-row hand fork with an off-vocabulary kind and a fixed badge, while the React route shows the 72-row shared ledger; make gen-rcore-revisions.mjs emit a pre-redacted public JSON from the same ledger and have the deck's in-flow two-click badge read it, gated in rcore-revisions and a deck-qa row that also runs on the live deck. (P0) Every pin, register row, domain JSON, ledger row and the generated module names r.165 while the bytes are r0.195 (and r0.178 has no row at all; revs 65–72 cite parent commits; drone-ledger.gen.ts was hand-copied): append honest rows for r.178 and r.195 as served-unregistered editions, release r.196 from r.178 with a patch whose base is named, bump both DECK_REV pins, regenerate the module, and add a `--check` to the generator. (P1) Own the share-link contract once (canonical play.html URL + range/mode) and probe both URL forms live; split the deck gates into a named CI step so a red gate is readable; hold BUILD.revision = header = DECK_REV. Keep the loop strip and the register design from r0.178; drop the r.180 plate enlargement, the r.193 fire-gate loosening, the r.195 R-CORE fork, the filled pose SVGs and the edited QA rows. The r0.178 screenshots' centred count card is not what af6f65e0's CSS produces (no media rule moves it) — render it headless before treating the bytes as the visual master. Sensor Fusion untouched.

### Odin (predictive & future-proof)

**verdict_on_current:** r0.195 (HEAD, frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html) is the end state of 36 direct hand edits of a 540 KB single file since 4bb4a3f7 (git log: every commit by the operator's account, none carried, hashed, patched or registered — register/README/HASHES/patches/DECK_REV all stop at r.165: deck-head.mjs:5 `DECK_REV = '165'`, deck-rev.ts:3, REVISIONS.md:48 last row r.165 "PENDING (Verify Live)"), while the one path that actually ships — Cloudflare's git build running `npm run build` — carries no deck gate at all (package.json:9 `prebuild` lists sign/sensor-fusion/financial tests only), so `gh run list` shows Deploy `failure` on every push from 040a21ba to 8f9b3540 (last success e522b2b3, 2026-10-08T23:21Z) and Verify Live red on every run, yet the site serves each commit. The gates were right and ignored: Verify Live on af6f65e0 passed "Poll live site" and "Probe /drone-2525/" and failed only at "Verify the served operator deck" because BUILD.revision was '0.166' (r178:652) under a header saying r0.178 (r178:318) against a pin of 165. The ledger was kept, but as prose: rev 61 `commit: PENDING` (the row that kills `test:ci` at traceability step 27 and hides every deck gate behind it, package.json:206 is one `&&` chain), rev 60 `shipped: PENDING`, revs 65–72 cite the parent commit (rev 72 "r.195" → 3380e7b; the r.195 code is 14a930d0), reversions filed as `release` (rev 66 "count is the center card" vs rev 68 "not in the center"; rev 62 "r.166 reinstated") though `correction` is in the vocabulary (traceability.test.mjs:30), and the deck's own QA was edited to agree with the code (cur:4743 order rewritten vs r178:4632; `qualKept` cur:4664 asserts the reverted QUAL; MARK_SURVIVES_THE_RETURN cur:4648–4654 still asserts r.162 behaviour r.166 reversed; row set frozen at 207 ids at r.165, r.178 and HEAD so none of r.166–r.195's behaviours has a row). r0.178 (af6f65e0) is a sound code base for the range — true-scale silMesh (r178:1026–1029, no `px<28`), pits drawn (r178:1886), strict roundOpen (r178:2514), the fire gate (r178:2047/2427/2515), IWQ_VI (r178:807–813) — but it already lacks the r.169 QUAL fix (r178:2509 `modeReturns` includes qual40; r178:2513 keepsMark excludes only 'bounce'; r178:875 rangeRelease keeps the mark), has no TRAIN DOWN clock, no pose card, and its R-CORE is only the loop text (r178:91/510/644). Prediction: with no patcher, no carried file and a shipping path that cannot go red, the same class recurs within days of any hand edit, whoever makes it.

**fixes:**

1. **The shipping path must be able to go red (deploy-with-a-red-gate class)**
   - *class:* pipeline fails open: the gate that is required is not the gate that ships
   - *files:* - frontend/package.json
     - .github/workflows/deploy.yml
     - .github/workflows/verify-live.yml
     - frontend/tests/deck-pipeline.test.mjs (new)
   - *evidence:* package.json:9 prebuild = lockcheck, integrity-guard, gen-seal-html, copy-pdf-worker, copy-migration, gen-i18n-index, sign-invariant, sign-lexicon-voice, sensor-fusion-r4a, financial-surface — no deck gate; deploy.yml:38-45 `gate` job runs test:ci, `ship` needs [gate, backend] (deploy.yml ship: `if: needs.gate.outputs.canbuild == 'true'`) but Cloudflare's git build deploys independently; `gh run list --workflow=deploy.yml`: failure on 040a21ba, 61b4d49d, 7ffd164e, 8206a28b, a146c120, 3ba0f97c, 5a6b64bb, 43a0dc46, 074ac55c, 3380e7b1, 14a930d0, 8f9b3540; last success e522b2b3 2026-10-08T23:21Z; verify-live.yml:113-118 the sha256-vs-register check exists and was red on every run; deploy.yml comment: 'a permanently red light teaches everyone to ignore the one that matters'
   - *why:* Every one of the 30 unregistered revisions reached phones through a door no gate guards; the operator tested regressions the repo had already named. Until the path that ships is the path that gates, every other fix below can be undone by one push.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* NEW frontend/tests/deck-pipeline.test.mjs in test:ci: asserts package.json `prebuild` contains `node tests/deck-consistency.test.mjs` and a plain-node served-equals-carried sha check (so Cloudflare's own build fails closed), and that deploy.yml `ship` still `needs: [gate, backend]`; plus Verify Live green on the ship SHA before the next revision (the no-outpacing law, CLAUDE.md Workflow Rules 1-2)
   - *approach:* Add the two plain-node deck checks to `prebuild` (deck-consistency.test.mjs is dependency-free; write `scripts/deck-served-check.mjs` that compares sha256(public/drone-2525/play.html) to the register's HEAD row). Protect `main`: work lands on claude/* branches, main advances by merge only when `gate` + `backend` are green and the previous ship's Verify Live is green. Split test:ci so the deck gates run as their own step and every red is named (today traceability's one red hides drone-playable, deck-consistency and deck-qa).
2. **Every exposure begins unmarked: TARGET → APPROVE → FIRE on every target in every mode (fire-gate class)**
   - *class:* authority outlives the exposure: a mark survives a target going down and rides back up red
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
   - *evidence:* cur:2619 `marksPersist(){ return chNum()===0&&!(lobby LIVE) }`; cur:2620 `modeReturns(){ return m==='bounce'||m==='qual40' }`; cur:2624 keepsMark excludes only `==='bounce'`; cur:956 rangeRelease `if(keepsMark(q)){ q.mist=false; return; }`; cur:2633 fireN HOLD 'TARGET_RETURNING … STILL RED · F WHEN IT STANDS'; identical at r178:2509-2513 and r178:875; the fix existed: b6487246 (r.169) `keepsMark(...)||state.rangeMode==='qual40') return false` and rangeTick `rangeRelease(q)` on every plate that stands ('a plate that just stood is unmarked'), wiped by b2dfa80a 'Reinstate the r.166 range' (−2,450 lines); cur:4664 QA `qualKept=!qq.up&&!!markSlotOf(qq.id)` asserts the reverted behaviour
   - *why:* The operator's whole purpose ('everyone targeting and approving targets before firing') is defeated in QUAL at r0.178 and at HEAD: a plate that rises in a later engagement is already RED and fires without TARGET or APPROVE. This is the invariant the 2v2/3v3 drone stage inherits.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* Deck QA row QUAL_FRESH_EVERY_EXPOSURE (replaces the `qualKept` half of MARKS_ARE_REMEMBERED cur:4655-4666): expose, mark, approve, let it fall, let it stand again → no slot, FIRE refused NO_RED_BOX; add to tests/deck-qa-manifest.mjs; drone-playable.test.mjs source regex that keepsMark names qual40 and rangeTick's stand-up path calls rangeRelease
   - *approach:* State the invariant in the file: 'a mark lives exactly as long as its exposure, in every mode'. Re-apply b6487246's two hunks on the r.178 base (keepsMark + rangeTick stand-up rangeRelease), keep r.166's TRAIN UP exclusion (cur:2624), make TRAIN DOWN's hit end the mark (already: modeReturns false), and delete the 'STILL RED · F WHEN IT STANDS' HOLD path (cur:2633) — one rule, no mode branch that keeps a mark.
3. **One predicate for 'the line may fire' (round-open class)**
   - *class:* two definitions of an armed range: roundOpen loosened without rangeArmed, so shots land while the light says WAIT and lapses do not count
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
   - *evidence:* cur:2625 roundOpen: `const intro=document.getElementById('intro'); return !!(intro&&intro.style.display==='none'&&ph!=='LIVE')` (074ac55c, r.193); rangeArmed cur:~884 still `ph==='PRACTICE'||ph==='LIVE'||!!state.qaArmed`; startLight returns 'R'/'O' when !rangeArmed(); r178:2514 roundOpen strict; ledger rev 69/70: 'The Range now opens in practice … The toast PRESS PRACTICE was the block'
   - *why:* r.193 fixed the symptom (a link-opened range could not fire) by weakening the r.135 rule instead of opening PRACTICE through the lobby door; the result is a range that fires on WAIT and books no lapse — exactly the class the fleet closed at r.133/r.135.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* Deck QA row NO_SHOT_ON_WAIT (intro hidden, lobby not PRACTICE → fireN REJECT ROUND_NOT_OPEN; after enterTheRange → PRACTICE → fires); drone-playable regex pins the r.178 roundOpen body and asserts `roundOpen()===rangeArmed()` is the single door
   - *approach:* Restore r178:2514 roundOpen; make enterTheRange (cur:5174) set the lobby to PRACTICE through the existing practice path (the same function the PRACTICE button calls), so a `?range=` link opens an ARMED line and the light, the lapse clock and the fire gate read one state.
4. **What is drawn is what is hit is what is boxed (true-scale class — the 'distorted targets')**
   - *class:* a render-only size override diverges from the hit-test and box geometry
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
   - *evidence:* cur:1131 silMesh: `const px=focalPx()*D.h/dist; if(px>0&&px<28){ const s=28/px; D={w:D.w*s,h:D.h*s}; } /* r.180 */` while plateRect (cur:~1150, r178:970) and slotBoxes/drawTbox keep plateDims; r178:1026-1029 silMesh has no scaling (`grep -c 'px<28'` = 0); 2960980f also removed `RANGE_WIRE.forEach` from the range branch (r178:1886 draws pits; cur:1913 only in the map branch); drone-playable.test.mjs:62 already requires `drawLaneLines(v,cam,W,H); RANGE_WIRE.forEach` and is red; operator shot8: silhouettes bunched/overlapping, lane boards stacked

The deck cannot import React, so the shared thing is the DATA and the RULES (placement, two-click, vocabulary), each gated.

12. **A durable deck pipeline: the single file is an OUTPUT, not the thing people edit (future-proofing)**
   - *class:* a 540 KB hand-edited artefact with no module boundary invites whole-file reversions
   - *files:* - frontend/scripts/build-deck.mjs (new)
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/src/ (new)
     - frontend/tests/deck-consistency.test.mjs

   - *why:* Every regression here was a whole-file edit. If the carried snapshot is produced by a deterministic assembler from small owned parts (range.js, qa.js, hud.css, poses/), a change is a diff to a part, the output hash still goes in the register, and a reversion is a visible revert of a part — the register stays byte-for-byte while the editing surface becomes reviewable.
   - *priority:* P2
   - *gate:* deck-consistency.test.mjs: rebuilding from src/ reproduces the carried HEAD bytes (sha equal); drone-playable's byte-identity (exists)
   - *approach:* After r.196 ships: extract the range sections into docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/src/*, an assembler that emits drone-2525_r.NNN.html deterministically, the register row records both the source-tree hash and the output hash; the patcher becomes a diff to src/.

**r178_screenshot_match:** Partly. The bytes: `git log -S'<span>r0.178</span>'` shows the header r0.178 existed only at af6f65e0 (added there, removed by 50826908), and Verify Live run 38075147721 on af6f65e0 passed 'Poll live site for af6f65e0…' and 'Probe /drone-2525/' (failing only at 'Verify the served operator deck' because r178:652 BUILD.revision '0.166' ≠ DECK_REV 165) — so the site served that commit's build and the shots' header/status text ('LAST · 150 M LEFT · DOWN', 'MAG 1 · 3 RDS', red '3', 'HIT · 150 M LEFT · TARGET · APPROVE · FIRE', amber 3D box then red plate) match af6f65e0's strings. The layout does not: r178:29 `#magWell{position:absolute;left:8px;top:92px;display:flex;gap:12px}` with r178:31-32 two 86 px cards min-height 112 and no width rule puts RELOAD at x≈8-94 and the count at x≈106-192 CSS px; r178:50 `#startLight{right:8px;top:42px}` puts the light 50 px ABOVE the cards; no @media (r178:154-200) or JS touches #magWell and no `left:50%` rule exists for it (r178 grep). The shots show RELOAD left, the count card CENTRED (≈45-55 % of width), the light on the SAME row, cards ≈56×70 CSS px. The nearest committed layout with a centred count is a146c120 (r.189) whose header reads r0.189. So the live page in the shots was most plausibly a working tree deployed by hand (deploy.yml:3-7 records hand `wrangler deploy` as the historic path; a dirty tree keeps HEAD's build stamp) — i.e. bytes that exist in no commit, which is itself the process finding. Settle it with one headless render of af6f65e0 at 428×926 before calling its bytes the visual master; until then: af6f65e0 = the code base, the operator's shots + words = the visual spec.

**minor_notes:** - GitHub evidence is reachable from this sandbox via `gh`: Deploy `failure` on every main push from 040a21ba (2026-10-11T01:24Z) through 8f9b3540; last `success` e522b2b3 2026-10-08T23:21Z; Verify Live `failure` on all four af6f65e0 runs and every run since, including the 30-minute schedule — four days of a red light nobody could act on.
- The one ledger row that kills `test:ci` is rev 61 (`commit: PENDING`, traceability.test.mjs:27); because package.json:206 chains every gate with `&&`, that single red hid drone-playable (111/4), deck-consistency (47/4) and deck-qa from every run — gate ordering is itself a defect class.
- r0.178's magazine svg box is 36×82 CSS px (r178:33) against a 48×96 viewBox (0.439 vs 0.5) and HEAD's is 24×50 (0.48); with the default preserveAspectRatio the drawing letterboxes rather than squashes (HEAD's only `preserveAspectRatio` is the QR, cur:5928) — the operator's 'distorted magazines' is the card shrink/re-proportion and position flips across r.183-r.191, so the gate is a measured card height + svg aspect, not a regex alone.
- RANGE_MENU_ON_SCREEN's order assertion differs (r178:4632 mode<restart<lane; cur:4743 mode<lane<restart) and shot1 shows TRAIN UP · LANE 21 … RESTART at right — the HEAD order matches the picture; verify by render before restoring r178's assertion.
- TRAIN DOWN: the operator says 'all 3' but the lane has 11 plates (TRAIN_ALL_UP cur:4477 asserts 11; downStart raises every plate) — a target-count setting (3 by default on the lane, N for the 3v3 challenge) is the class fix; ask him before changing the count.
- IWQ_VI phase 1 `pos` is one string 'STANDING → PRONE UNSUPPORTED' (cur:886); qualStance already splits it by engagement index — the pose card must read the index, never the string.
- The plan file /root/.claude/plans/do-me-a-fsvor-pure-wirth.md changed on disk during this pass (other lenses appending); nothing here depends on the new content.
- Sensor Fusion (frontend/app/SensorFusion-2525: ledger.ts + sensor-fusion.tsx:105 mounting the shared RCoreBadge) is untouched by this plan and is the pattern to cite: its own ledger, the shared badge — built with the Grok bot and working.
- Verify Live's register sha check (verify-live.yml:116-118) is the right instrument; it only needs a register that moves and a path that cannot deploy past it.

**summary:** Odin's reading: the range did not regress because of one bad line but because the repo let a 540 KB single file be hand-edited thirty times outside its register while the only door that ships (Cloudflare's `npm run build`, no deck gate in `prebuild`) could not go red — so every gate that would have caught r.180's enlarged plates, r.193's open round, the twice-used numbers and the forked R-CORE was red and ignored for four days. Restore af6f65e0 as the code base (true scale, pits, strict round, the gate), re-apply the one fix r.178 never had (b6487246: every QUAL exposure begins unmarked), keep three things born after it (the TRAIN DOWN clock — put on the record; the cold-range end; the pose-card drivers), drop the rest, and ship r.196 through the discipline that stopped at r.165: one patcher of asserted edits, the carried file + sha + register row + ledger `correction` row + pins in one commit, bookkeeping after Verify Live. Then make recurrence impossible, not discouraged: the deck gates in Cloudflare's own prebuild, main protected behind a green gate, a bookkeeping gate that turns red on the next hand edit of play.html, a `--check` on the generated ledger, and the shared R-CORE data in the deck. Grok tests — his findings become time-stamped ask files and headless/phone runs, never writes. Credit where due: Sensor Fusion works, was built with the Grok bot, and is the pattern (own ledger, shared badge) the deck should copy.

### Pangu (cutting-edge)

**verdict_on_current:** Through the pose-raster lens neither revision delivers ask 3. r0.178 (af6f65e0) has NO pose at all: the only position cue is the phase toast 'PHASE n · <pos> · PRESS RELOAD · MOVE' (r178:960) and the strip text (r178:2472); its HUD row is two 86-px magazine cards with min-height 112 at left 8/top 92 (r178:29-36) and the light at top 42 (r178:50), so there is no card the pose could be 'the same height as'. r0.195 (HEAD) has a pose well, but it is an illustration outside the deck's own law: five inline SVGs that are FILLED (fill="currentColor", cur:453-457), painted in five colours of which three (#8AA0AE, #7AA4C4, #9AA8B4, cur:52-54) are not in the deck's T13 (cur:728), in a 58×52 square (cur:46) that is neither square nor the magazine card's height (~66 px from cur:35/38/39) nor the light's (~74 px from cur:79/81/84/87), with the word OUTSIDE the square (cur:451), at a hand-typed left:158px (cur:43), five DOM nodes toggled by .on (cur:47-48) plus a .done class with no rule (cur:1013). No raster, no generator, no gate: vector-law.test.mjs:16 judges four React files and never play.html, and no deck QA row or test names the pose. What IS right at HEAD and must be carried: the engagement-driven mapping qualStance() (cur:1012: STANDING only while engagement 1 is up, PRONE UNSUPPORTED for 2-5, phase 2/3/4 → poses 2/3/4, the NEXT pose during the phase gap, 'RANGE IS COLD' when done) and the five words in the operator's order (cur:1013) — but it is an impure function reading state/rangeRun, so no gate can prove the 18-row table. The 7.77 MB ceiling (drone-playable.test.mjs:13,17) is a non-issue: the deck is 547,290 B and five 2× one-bit rasters add ~4-18 KB.

**fixes:**

1. **Replace the five filled, five-colour inline SVG poses with ONE deterministic generator: stroke-only SVG source → 1-bit PNG at 2× → data URIs in the deck**
   - *class:* Hand-drawn art inside the deck with no source of truth, no colour law and no reproducibility (an illustration pasted into HTML instead of a generated asset)
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/scripts/gen-pose-rasters.mjs (new)
     - frontend/lib/drone-2525/poses.ts (new)
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/poses/ (new: pose-0-standing.svg/.png … pose-4-standing-supported.svg/.png)
     - frontend/tests/drone-poses.test.mjs (new)
     - frontend/tests/vector-law.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* cur:453-457 `<g fill="currentColor">` on all five poses (filled silhouettes, 72×56 viewBox); cur:50-54 five colours — `#posWell i[data-p="2"]{color:#8AA0AE}` / `"3" #7AA4C4` / `"4" #9AA8B4` are absent from the deck's 13 at cur:728 `const T13={VOID:'#000000',PANEL:'#070B10',STROKE:'#1C2A3A',WIRE:'#7AB8C4',ROAD:'#4A6A78',WATER:'#2A6D88',DOME:'#C9A227',SI:'#E8D5B0',HI:'#5BA8FF',AI:'#C084FC',LOCK:'#E24B3B',TAG:'#3DCC8A',GIMBAL:'#F0A020'}`; vector-law.ts:5-6 'Lines only — no fill … One saturated stroke colour per object, from the 13'; vector-law.test.mjs:16 SURFACES = four React files, play.html never judged; r178:960 the only r0.178 position cue is a toast; the deck's only inline image precedent is the QR GIF at cur:7638 `'data:image/gif;base64,'`; package.json carries only @playwright/test (no sharp/resvg) and 'playwright not importable' in CI (plan B).
   - *why:* Ask 3 verbatim: 'mini poses are raster simplifications 1983 Star Wars video game style with words' under the vector law (black ground, one stroke colour of the 13, no fill). Today's poses break every clause (fill, five colours, three off-palette) and are unreproducible hand edits — the exact class that produced r.166-r.195. A generator makes the five rasters a pure function of a small polyline source, so the art can be regenerated, hashed and gated; rendering in pure Node (own Bresenham onto a 70×70 cell grid at 2 px per cell → 140×140, 1-bit indexed PNG with palette {#000000, ONE T13 hex}, stored-deflate IDAT with adler32/crc32 computed in the script) needs no browser, no native module and no zlib version in the loop, so the bytes are identical on every machine ('the signer never meets the machine'). Each pose is ~0.5-3.6 KB base64; five together < 20 KB against the 7.77 MB ceiling.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* NEW frontend/tests/drone-poses.test.mjs in test:ci: (1) import the generator, regenerate the five PNGs in memory and assert the five data URIs found in BOTH public/drone-2525/play.html and the carried docs/…/drone-2525_r.<DECK_REV>.html equal them byte-for-byte; (2) decode each PNG (inflate IDAT, unfilter) and assert exactly two colours {#000000, X} with X one hex lifted from the deck's own T13 line (range-2525.test.mjs:16-21 lift pattern) and the SAME X in all five; (3) each data URI ≤ 6 KB, five ≤ 30 KB, served file ≤ FILE_BUDGET (drone-playable.test.mjs:13); (4) the SVG sources carry `fill="none"`, one stroke hex, `shape-rendering="crispEdges"`, no gradient/filter/text-shadow; (5) EXTEND vector-law.test.mjs with a deck clause: the `#posWell` block of play.html contains no `fill="currentColor"`/`fill="#`; (6) gen-pose-rasters.mjs --check exits 1 when committed .svg/.png/data URIs differ from a fresh generation (the --check gen-rcore-revisions.mjs lacks).
   - *approach:* poses.ts declares the five figures as integer polylines on a 70×70 cell grid traced from his TC 3-22.9 figures with the fewest strokes that read at 70 CSS px: helmet (8-gon), one body polyline, one rifle line, one support stroke where the figure has one (sandbag rect for PRONE SUPPORTED, post for KNEELING SUPPORTED, wall for STANDING SUPPORTED, none for STANDING / PRONE UNSUPPORTED); colour = one T13 constant (T13.DOME #C9A227, the magazine frame's gold, so the HUD row reads as one object set); words = the five operator strings in order. gen-pose-rasters.mjs emits for the record docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/poses/pose-N-<word>.svg (stroke-only, the human-readable source) and .png (140×140, 1-bit indexed), prints the `const POSE_PNG=[…]` data-URI table, and the rNNN patch script splices that table into play.html (the register's way: patches/r*_to_r*.py); the five .svg/.png are listed in HASHES_rNNN beside the deck. The deck draws the raster with `image-rendering:pixelated` so the 2× bitmap on a 3× phone stays crisp (bilinear filtering would manufacture a 14th colour at display time).
2. **One HUD card height, derived not typed: the pose card is a rounded square of the magazine card's height, the light the same, the word inside the card**
   - *class:* Card geometry hand-typed per card in px (three cards, three heights, one of them not even square) instead of one declared dimension every card derives from

Operator addendum 3: 'same height as magazines and stop light accept a rounded square'; addendum 9: the magazine drawing keeps 48:96 at every size. Shot1/shot2 (428 CSS px): RELOAD ≈ 56×70 at top ≈ 42, count card content-sized (~39 px) and centred at x≈214, light ≈ 44×77 on the same row.
   - *why:* The acceptance criterion the operator wrote is a geometric equality across three cards; while each card's height is the sum of its own padding/svg/font rules, the equality is accidental and any later font or padding edit (the r.183-r.191 'card sizes' churn) silently breaks it again. One custom property makes equality structural, and sizing the magazine svg FROM the card height with width = half keeps the drawing undistorted (addendum 9's law) — the same mechanism the pose card uses to be a square.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* NEW boot-QA row HUD_CARDS_ONE_HEIGHT in the deck (added to DECK_QA_ROWS in tests/deck-qa-manifest.mjs:4 so drone-deck-qa.mjs:41-44 holds the manifest equal) measuring getBoundingClientRect of #magReload, #magWell .body, #posWell .card and #startLight in QUAL with a target up: all four heights equal ±1 px; pose card width == height ±1; the magazine <svg> width/height = 0.5 ± 0.02; no pair of the four rects overlaps; all four inside innerWidth; the note carries the four rects (measured, not asserted — drone-deck-qa.mjs:52 style regex). Run at drone-deck-qa.mjs:26 VIEWPORTS plus a 320×568 phone. EXTEND START_LIGHT_CLEAR (cur:4730) so its `clash` list includes #posWell.
   - *approach:* `#stage{--hudCard:70px}` (the operator's shot height); `#magWell button,#magWell .body,#posWell .card,#startLight{height:var(--hudCard);box-sizing:border-box}`; `#posWell .card{width:var(--hudCard);border-radius:14px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between}` holding `<img id="posImg">` + `<b id="posName">` (two-line wrap for the two-word names, same 8-px label rule as RELOAD); `#magWell svg{height:calc(var(--hudCard) - 24px);width:calc((var(--hudCard) - 24px)/2)}`; the HUD row becomes one flex row (RELOAD · count · pose · light, `justify-content:space-between`) instead of four absolute lefts, so the pose card can never collide with the light at 320 px; `#posImg{image-rendering:pixelated;width:calc(var(--hudCard) - 26px);height:auto}`.

**r178_screenshot_match:** NO — af6f65e0 does not render as shot1/shot2, and no commit does. (1) The header `<b>eXeL</b><span>r0.178` exists ONLY at af6f65e0: checked across all 34 play.html commits since 4bb4a3f7 — every other commit's header reads its own number (dc495fe6 r0.177, 50826908 r0.179, … 60d5a411 r0.184, 6a4097f6 r0.186, 8206a28b r0.188, a146c120 r0.189, 5a6b64bb r0.191, 14a930d0 r0.195), so the photographed JS is af6f65e0's. (2) af6f65e0's only #magWell rules are r178:29-37 (`left:8px;top:92px … gap:12px`, cards `width:86px`, `min-height:112px`, `svg{width:36px;height:82px}`) and the light is `right:8px;top:42px` (r178:50; the @media blocks at :119-213 touch #startLight only, :182) — two 86×112 cards 50 px BELOW the light. (3) The shots at 428 CSS px (1284 px / DPR 3) show RELOAD ≈ 56×70 CSS px with its top at ≈ 42 (the light's row), the count card CONTENT-SIZED (~39 px, narrower than RELOAD) and centred at x ≈ 214, the drawing ≈ 18×38, the light ≈ 44×77 on the same row. (4) Nearest committed layouts: 6a4097f6 (r.186) has 52-px cards with max-height 72 and an 18×36 drawing (the drawing size matches) but cards at top 62 under a light at top 42 and no centring; a146c120 (r.189) centres the count and puts the light on the same row (top 78) but with 70-px cards and a 32×66 drawing. So the live r0.178 he photographed was af6f65e0's JS with a CSS the repo never carried (deploy.yml records hand `wrangler deploy`s). Treat the shot as the visual master for the HUD row and af6f65e0 as the behavioural master; a headless render of af6f65e0 at 428×926 is still worth putting on the record beside the shot, but it will not match.

**minor_notes:** - 'The 13' inside play.html is the deck's T13 (cur:728: VOID/PANEL/STROKE/WIRE/ROAD/WATER/DOME/SI/HI/AI/LOCK/TAG/GIMBAL) — NOT lib/wire-core palette.ts's ALLOWED_HEX (TRINITY_COLORS IR→ROYGBIV→UV→white). A pose gate that read trinity-palette.ts would fail on every colour the deck already draws (#C9A227, #E24B3B, #3DCC8A…); lift T13 from the deck instead. Recording that the deck and the React surfaces use two different 13s is a finding for the vector-law lens.
- Words in HTML, not pixels: put the five words in the card's <b> (the same 8-px label rule RELOAD and the count use, two-line wrap for the two-word names) and keep the PNG a figure-only bitmap. At 56-70 px card width a baked bitmap font would be ~3 px per glyph and unreadable; HTML text is also the accessible form. Record as a decision; if the operator wants the words IN the raster, the generator owns a 3×5 pixel font and the gate's two-colour check still holds.
- image-rendering:pixelated on the one <img> is part of the law, not polish: a 140-px bitmap on a DPR-3 phone is bilinear-filtered to 210 px and every edge pixel becomes a shade — a 14th colour manufactured at display time.
- Stored-deflate IDAT (level 0, adler32/crc32 in the script) makes the PNG bytes a pure function of the polylines on any Node; ~2.7 KB per 140×140 1-bit image, ~3.6 KB base64, ~18 KB for five. Real deflate would be ~0.5 KB each but ties the hash to a zlib build; either is far under the ceiling (547,290 B served vs 7,770,000).
- The operator's five TC 3-22.9 figures (shot3-7, 188-333 KB JPEGs) are REFERENCE for tracing, never embedded: the per-asset budget in fix 1's gate is what stops a 1.4 MB 'quick fix' of inlining them.
- paintPos() runs on every paintCues() (15 call sites) with a querySelectorAll over five nodes; with one <img> and a change guard it becomes one string compare.
- The `.done` class toggled at cur:1013 has no CSS rule — dead state; drop with the five-node markup.
- During the phase gap qualStance() shows the NEXT phase's pose (cur:1012 `R.phase==='phasegap' → Math.min(4,ph+1)`) — correct for the operator's 'move' cue; the gate in fix 3 should assert it rather than let a future edit 'fix' it to the previous pose.
- Mid-phase reload in QUAL is allowed by magReload (cur:917-921 refuses only full or a fifth) so mag.n can run ahead of the phase; the pose follows the ENGAGEMENT (the target), the magazine card follows the shooter — fix 3's gate asserts the program's mapping, not the shooter's count.
- Sensor Fusion (app/SensorFusion-2525) untouched by every fix here; it keeps its own ledger.ts and was built with the Grok bot — credit stands.

**summary:** Pose-raster lens: r0.178 has no pose (a toast, r178:960); r0.195 has five FILLED inline SVGs in five colours, three off the deck's own T13, in a 58×52 non-square box with the word outside and a hand-typed left:158px — unreproducible art with no generator and no gate (vector-law.test.mjs never reads play.html). Six class fixes: (P0) one deterministic generator — polyline source on a 70×70 cell grid → stroke-only SVG for the record → 140×140 1-bit PNG (own Bresenham, stored-deflate, palette {black, one T13 hex}) → five data URIs spliced by the register's patch, gated by a new drone-poses.test.mjs that regenerates and compares bytes and decoded pixels and enforces a per-asset budget; (P0) one --hudCard custom property so the pose card is a rounded SQUARE of the magazine card's height, the light the same, the magazine svg derived at exactly half-width, image-rendering:pixelated, one flex row, gated by a measured deck row HUD_CARDS_ONE_HEIGHT at 320/390/844; (P1) POSES and POSE_BY_ENG declared beside IWQ_ENG and lifted by range-2525.test.mjs ([0,1,1,1,1,2×5,3×4,4×4], MAG 1/2/3/4 ↔ poses) plus a deck row stepping all 18 engagements; (P1) one <img> swapped from the table instead of five DOM copies; (P1) every deck image inline (the r.195 /r-core/ icon is the precedent to close); (P1) the ten pose files hashed in HASHES_rNNN beside the carried deck with --check in CI. Budget is a non-issue (+≤18 KB on 547 KB vs 7.77 MB). The operator's r0.178 screenshots cannot be produced by af6f65e0's CSS (86×112 cards at top 92 vs the shot's ~56×70 cards on the light's row with the count centred) nor by any commit — af6f65e0 is the behavioural master, the shot the visual one.

### Sofia (multi-perspective)

**verdict_on_current:** Seen from five seats at once, r0.195 contradicts itself and r0.178 is the right base but not a finished state. The operator on his phone sees targets drawn larger than they can be hit (silMesh cur:1131 scales any plate under 28 px while plateRect cur:1069–1074 / slotBoxes / drawTbox keep true size), the firing pits gone, a magazine row that is not his picture (cur:33–41 vs shot 1), and an R-CORE reticle fixed over the dock (cur:68). The trainee hears two rules for one act: a TRAIN UP hit says 'TARGET · APPROVE · FIRE' (cur:2668, bounce only) while a QUAL hit says 'STILL RED · F WHEN IT STANDS' (cur:2633, 2668 first branch) — because keepsMark (cur:2624) excludes only bounce and modeReturns (cur:2620) includes qual40, so a QUAL plate that rises again is already red and fires without TARGET or APPROVE; r0.178 has the identical gap (r178:2509, 2513, 2522, 875). The pilot/targeteer pair already lives under the operator's rule — in a LIVE room marksPersist() is false (r178:2508/cur:2619) and approval is per shot by the other seat (approveDesig TWO_HUMANS) — so solo play is the outlier, not the room. The reviewer reading R-CORE finds a hand-typed 5-row RCORE_HIST with a kind 'approved' that no vocabulary knows (cur:709–715 vs revisions.ts:38, traceability.test.mjs:30), a hard-coded CLOSE (cur:697), and a ledger that has no row for r0.178 at all (rev 60 r.165 → rev 61 r.176 → rev 62 r.166 → rev 63 r0.186), cites parent commits on revs 65–72 (rev 72 '3380e7b' is r.194's code), and leaves rev 60/61 PENDING; the React route meanwhile mounts the shared badge from that ledger (command-ux1.tsx:36–38,147), so one surface shows two histories. The CI sees every deck gate red since 2026-10-08 (DECK_REV='165' deck-head.mjs:5 vs BUILD '0.195' cur:730; drone-playable:18,22,62; deck-consistency:22,29; traceability:27) and deploys anyway because prebuild (package.json:9) carries none of them while test:ci (package.json:206) does — and the deck's own QA rows (MARK_SURVIVES_THE_RETURN cur:4648–4654, qualKept cur:4664) assert the reverted behaviour, at r0.178 as well (r178:4541, 4553). r0.178 (af6f65e0) is the master for behaviour — true-scale plates, pits drawn, the r.135 fire gate, the magazine drawings, the Table VI program — but its CSS is not the operator's picture and its QUAL fire gate is not his rule.

**fixes:**

1. **One fire rule for every standing target, every mode, solo = room: a target that stands (or stands again) is unmarked until TARGET → amber → APPROVE → red → FIRE**
   - *class:* mark persistence across a target's fall and return (keepsMark / modeReturns / marksPersist / rangeRelease / the fireN and approveDesig HOLD paths)
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/patches/r195_to_r196.py
     - frontend/tests/deck-qa-manifest.mjs
     - frontend/tests/drone-playable.test.mjs
     - frontend/tests/range-2525.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* r178:2509 `modeReturns(){ … return m==='bounce'||m==='qual40'; }` · r178:2513 `keepsMark(q){ if(!q||!q.form||!marksPersist()||!modeReturns()||(state.rangeMode||'bounce')==='bounce') return false; …}` (only TRAIN UP drops the mark) · r178:2522 fireN: `decide('HOLD',…,{reason:'TARGET_RETURNING'}); toast(… 'IS COMING BACK UP · STILL RED · F WHEN IT STANDS')` · r178:875 rangeRelease: `if(keepsMark(q)){ q.mist=false; return; }` (a rising plate keeps its red) · cur:2620, 2624, 2633, 957 identical · cur:2668 the hit sentence 'TARGET · APPROVE · FIRE' only `if((state.rangeMode||'bounce')==='bounce')` · cur:4664 `const qualKept=!qq.up&&!!markSlotOf(qq.id)` asserts the gap; cur:4648–4654 MARK_SURVIVES_THE_RETURN asserts `back=…phase==='red'` in TRAIN UP, contradicting cur:2624 (same at r178:4541) · room already per-shot: r178:2508 `marksPersist(){ return chNum()===0&&!(state.lobby&&state.lobby.phase==='LIVE'); }`, approveDesig TWO_HUMANS · the r.169 fix existed (b6487246) and was wiped by b2dfa80a
   - *why:* Ask (4) verbatim: every target in every mode needs the user to Target, Approve and Fire; amber when targeted, red after approval — 'to simulate a drone swarm one day that has everyone targeting and approving targets before firing'. Today a QUAL plate in a later engagement comes back already red and fires on F alone (solo), while the same plate in a room needs the other seat — the demo of the swarm rule contradicts itself on one phone. Fixing only QUAL (the instance) repeats b6487246; the class is 'a mark outlives its target'.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* Deck QA row EVERY_TARGET_NEEDS_TARGET_APPROVE_FIRE run in TRAIN UP, TRAIN DOWN and QUAL 40 (F on a standing unmarked target → REJECT NO_RED_BOX, no round; TARGET → amber box; F → AMBER_NO_APPROVE; APPROVE → red; F → HIT; the returned/lapsed/re-exposed plate is unmarked and F refused) + MARKS_ARE_REMEMBERED re-stated (the hit target's mark ends, the others stay; qualKept → !markSlotOf) + manifest; drone-playable.test.mjs regex that keepsMark returns false unconditionally (or modeReturns is bounce-only) and that MARK_SURVIVES_THE_RETURN is absent; range-2525.test.mjs lifts keepsMark/rangeRelease and proves a rising plate has no slot
   - *approach:* Remove rather than add: make keepsMark() return false for every mode (delete its true branches), so rangeRelease always releases, the fireN/approveDesig TARGET_RETURNING HOLD paths become dead code and are removed, modeReturns keeps only the 'stands again' meaning (bounce) — exactly the r.169 shape. One sentence after every hit in every mode: 'HIT · <plate> · TARGET · APPROVE · FIRE'. State the invariant as a comment at keepsMark. Red-first: run the new row against r0.178 and r0.195 bytes and record the failure.

A 300 m plate drawn ~4.5× its size at 1× invites shots outside the true outline that miss, and the amber/red voxel can be smaller than the drawn plate — the trainee cannot trust what he sees; the r.138/r.141 true-scale rule is broken while its own gate stays green.

**r178_screenshot_match:** NO — af6f65e0's CSS cannot render the operator's shots 1–2. r178:29–34 give `#magWell{left:8px;top:92px;gap:12px}` with `button,.body{width:86px;min-height:112px}` and `svg 36×82` — two EQUAL 86 px cards side by side at the left — and `#startLight{top:42px}` (r178:50, :182) ABOVE the row. Shot 1 measured at 1284 px = 428 CSS px: RELOAD card x≈8–64 (56 px wide), count card x≈194–233 (39 px, centred on 214 = 428/2), light x≈377–419 (42 px), all three tops ≈214 px, heights ≈68 px — two different card widths cannot come from one `width:86px` rule, and the light sits ON the row. No commit among the 28 I traced (47f66e21 → 14a930d0) pairs a `<span>r0.178</span>` header with a centred count; the only centred-count CSS is a146c120 (r0.189: `#magWell{left:0;right:0;height:0}`, `.body{left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%)}`, `#magReload{left:8px}`, width 70, light top 78) with header r0.189. A second tell: the shot's TRAIN UP · LANE 21 … RESTART order is what 60d5a411 (r.184) wrote into the QA row (mode < lane < RESTART), while r178:4632 asserts mode < RESTART < lane. Timing fits a served r0.178 (af6f65e0 at 03:02 UTC 2026-10-10, the next commit 50826908 at 21:51 UTC; the shots at 04:40/04:41 phone time), but deploy.yml:3–8 records that hand `wrangler deploy`s are the only Source in Deployment History — the served r0.178 may never have been committed as such. What DOES match af6f65e0: the header r0.178, 'LAST · 150 M LEFT · DOWN', 'MAG 1 · 3 RDS · ZOOM 4.8×/30×', the red '3' (round colours), 'AMBER · T2 · 200 M RIGHT · PRESS APPROVE', the amber voxel and red reticle, TARGET/APPROVE/FIRE buttons, the gold magFrame and cyan RELOAD magazines at 1:2. Conclusion: the code base is af6f65e0, the visual master is the picture; settle it by rendering af6f65e0 at 428×926 headless beside shot 1 in the r.196 notes before any CSS is written.

**minor_notes:** - Correction to exploration finding C: the RANGE_MENU_ON_SCREEN order row (`on[0].left<on[2].left&&on[2].left<on[1].left`) was rewritten by 60d5a411 (r.184), not by r.195 (`git diff 3380e7b1 14a930d0` does not touch it), and it measures mode/RESTART/lane, not the magazine row; its order matches the operator's picture — keep it, do not restore r178:4632.
- The deck-qa gate was already red at r0.178: r178:4541 `back=…phase==='red'` and r178:4553 qualKept assert behaviour that keepsMark (false in bounce since r.166) no longer has. 'Last known working' means on his phone, not in CI — restore the behaviour, re-state the gates.
- The operator wrote 'how fast to target all 3' for TRAIN DOWN; the lane raises 11 (cur:4477 TRAIN_ALL_UP). Make the count one declared constant and ask — a genuine choice that is his.
- The deck has no lexicon; any R-CORE words inside it are English only — stage the rcore.* keys in AFTER_FILL for the ×33 law and say so in the notes (the link-out approach avoids the question entirely).
- rcore-revisions.test.mjs:107 guards only the React badge source; nothing in test:ci reads play.html for a fixed R-CORE overlay — the new deck-rcore gate is what makes the shared rule bind the deck.
- The ledger's KINDS already accept 'correction' (traceability.test.mjs:30; three correction rows exist), so rev 73 as a correction passes; 'approved' (cur:710) does not.
- drone-ledger.gen.ts (537 lines, 22 `shipped` keys) was hand-copied; gen-rcore-revisions.mjs:52–71 never writes `shipped` and has no --check flag — regenerate and add the check to test:ci.
- The QR link form changed from `/drone-2525/play.html?range=` (r178:7568) to `/drone-2525/play?range=` (cur:7703); Verify Live fetches only play.html (verify-live.yml:111) — whether the static host resolves the extension-less path is unverified from the sandbox (proxy 403); fetch the exact QR URL in Verify Live.
- Sensor Fusion (app/SensorFusion-2525, its own ledger.ts) works and was built with the Grok bot — credit it in the fleet record and the r.196 notes; no fix above touches it.
- Pose colours at HEAD: #8AA0AE, #7AA4C4, #9AA8B4 are not in the deck's T13 (cur:728); the deck's own `OK` palette set (cur:729) guards canvas strokes only, not CSS — the pose-rasters gate should check the PNG bytes' colours, which closes that hole.
- The pose card should not be aria-hidden (cur:450): on the range the position word is content a trainee and a screen reader both need.

**summary:** Through five seats — the operator on his phone, a trainee, a pilot/targeteer pair, a reviewer reading R-CORE, and the CI — the current deck contradicts itself: targets drawn bigger than they are hit, two fire rules (TRAIN UP re-marks, QUAL keeps red), a START not on the record, a pose card in a fourth shape and three off-palette colours, a forked 5-row R-CORE fixed over the dock, and every deck gate red since 2026-10-08 while Cloudflare deployed each push. Ten fixes, by class: (P0) one fire rule for every standing target in every mode, solo = room (keepsMark false everywhere; the r.169 shape); (P0) one geometry per target and the pits drawn; (P0) the served deck becomes a carried, hashed, registered revision and prebuild carries the deck gates so a red gate fails the Cloudflare build; (P0) the range's R-CORE is the shared badge/panel/ledger/words — smallest change: the second click opens the shared React panel from the same ledger, otherwise a vanilla port under a parity gate; (P1) TRAIN DOWN's clock as decide rows; (P1) five stroke-only raster poses with their words, one at a time, a rounded square of the magazine's measured height, driven by the engagement and the magazine; (P1) the picture as measured master (RELOAD left, count centred, light right, 48:96 never squashed); (P1) one predicate for 'the line is hot' and every entry through enterTheRange; (P2) QA rows re-stated to the rule; (P2) ledger rows corrected by append. Keep af6f65e0 as the behaviour base; keep after it the r.182 timer, the r.179/181 cold range, the pose logic and words, enterTheRange/QR, the r.189 centred model and the r.184 menu order; drop the r.180 enlargement, the r.193 loosening, the r.195 fork, the filled multi-colour SVGs and the r.191 card CSS. The screenshots do not render from af6f65e0's CSS (two unequal card widths and the light on the row cannot come from r178:29–34, :50) — the picture is the master, settled by a headless render first.

### Thor (risk & security stress)

**verdict_on_current:** Under stress the fire gate is not one gate. At r0.195 (tree) and at r0.178 (af6f65e0) a QUAL plate that stands again is already RED: modeReturns() includes qual40 (r178:2509 / cur:2620), keepsMark() keeps the shooter's own mark in qual40 solo (r178:2513 / cur:2624), rangeRelease() returns early on a kept mark (r178:875 / cur:957), and IWQ_VI reuses the same bases across engagements (r178:807–812: C-300 in eng 5/7/8/9/10, C-150L in 3/5/10/14), so fireN() HOLDs 'TARGET_RETURNING' then fires with no new TARGET/APPROVE when the plate rises — the deck's own QA row enshrines it (`qualKept=!qq.up&&!!markSlotOf(qq.id)`, cur:4664). r0.195 then forked the round predicate: roundOpen() (cur:2625) opens the round whenever the intro is hidden and the room is not LIVE, while rangeArmed() (cur:965) still says PRACTICE/LIVE/QA — a WAITING room fires, the light shows WAIT (cur:989) and lapses are not booked (cur:1059); the boot QA row NO_FIRE_BEFORE_THE_ROUND (cur:4600) stays green only because the intro is up when it runs. silMesh (cur:1130) draws any plate under 28 px up to 4.5× its true size in every range mode while plateRect/plateHit/pipOn/reticleNear (cur:1069–1085, 2192, 2184) hit on the true rect — the picture invites a shot the gate refuses ("targets don't work"). A fixed R-CORE badge (cur:68, z-index 9) sits on the dock's own bottom offset (cur:161, z-index 6); two taps open a full-screen sheet (cur:70, z-index 70) while rangeTick keeps running (openRcoreHist cur:721–726 touches no state). On the wire, a peer MISS row marks my plate engaged with no approval check (cur:2273 → cur:2594 'ONE ROUND PER TARGET' refuses my shot), a peer LAPSE row downs my plate and books an UNFIRED MISS from any sender (cur:2274), and a peer HIT needs only ANY APPROVE row for that id in the whole session (cur:2265) — r0.178 carries the same three lines (r178:2154/2164/2165). TARGET/APPROVE refusals are toasts, not rows (cur:2160–2163, 2540–2543; r178:2049–2052, 2429). TRAIN DOWN START bypasses decide() (cur:5314). And every one of these shipped with the deck gates red: deploy.yml's gate job runs test:ci (deploy.yml:76) and ship needs it (:201–203), but the git-connected Workers build deploys on push regardless and `prebuild` (frontend/package.json:9) carries none of the deck gates; DECK_REV is pinned at '165' (frontend/tests/deck-head.mjs:5) so range-2525 reads a dead carried file (range-2525.test.mjs:13 `deckUrl()`) while drone-playable's byte-identity check (:18) is red and ignored. r0.178 is the right code base — its fire gate (roundOpen ≡ rangeArmed, r178:2514/883), true-scale plates, drawn pits (r178:1886) and the r.148 HIT-row guard (r178:2154) are intact — but it is not clean: it has the QUAL kept-mark gap, BUILD.revision '0.166' under a r0.178 header (r178:652 vs :318), and the same wire gaps.

**fixes:**

1. **A mark never outlives its exposure — every standing target needs its own TARGET → APPROVE → FIRE (QUAL red-mark-persists)**
   - *class:* Authority persistence across exposures: an approval granted for one exposure is spent by that exposure; a target that stands again is unmarked in every mode
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
     - frontend/tests/deck-qa-manifest.mjs
     - frontend/tests/drone-playable.test.mjs
     - frontend/tests/range-2525.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* r178:2509 / cur:2620 `function modeReturns(){ … return m==='bounce'||m==='qual40'; }` · r178:2513 / cur:2624 `keepsMark(q)` returns true for an own mark when marksPersist()&&modeReturns() and mode!=='bounce' · r178:875 / cur:957 `rangeRelease(q){ … if(keepsMark(q)){ q.mist=false; return; }` · cur:2634 fireN: `if(…ref.up===false…&&keepsMark(ref)){ decide('HOLD',…'TARGET_RETURNING'); toast('… STILL RED · F WHEN IT STANDS') }` then at cur:2636 FIRE passes with `state.desig.phase==='red'` untouched · r178:807–812 IWQ_VI reuses bases (C-300 eng 5,7,8,9,10; C-150L eng 3,5,10,14; C-250 eng 5,9,10,13,16,18) and cur:1049 re-raises them with `_eng=false` · cur:4664 QA row `const qualKept=!qq.up&&!!markSlotOf(qq.id)` asserts the gap; cur:4648 MARK_SURVIVES_THE_RETURN asserts the pre-r.166 TRAIN UP · b6487246 (r.169) fixed it, b2dfa80a wiped it
   - *why:* Ask (4) in its own words: in EVERY mode every target needs TARGET → APPROVE → FIRE — the swarm rehearsal is that nobody fires on a stale approval. Today in QUAL phase 2–4 a shooter who approved C-300 once fires it four more times with no human in the loop, and the deck's own gate says that is correct.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs + tests/deck-qa-manifest.mjs: retire MARK_SURVIVES_THE_RETURN, rewrite MARKS_ARE_REMEMBERED (hit target's mark ENDS, others stay, all three modes, `qualKept` → `!markSlotOf`), add EVERY_TARGET_NEEDS_TARGET_APPROVE_FIRE (TRAIN UP / TRAIN DOWN / QUAL: F on a returned or re-raised plate → NO_RED_BOX row, no round) and QUAL_EVERY_TARGET_NEEDS_APPROVAL (expose C-300 in eng 5, approve, lapse, re-expose in eng 7 → unmarked, F refused); frontend/tests/drone-playable.test.mjs regex: `modeReturns` returns `m==='bounce'` only; frontend/tests/range-2525.test.mjs lifts keepsMark/modeReturns/rangeRelease and proves them without a browser. Written red-first against af6f65e0 and 8f9b3540.
   - *approach:* Restore r.169 on the r.178 base: modeReturns() → TRAIN UP only; keepsMark() false in qual40; rangeRelease() clears desig + slot whenever a plate goes down or is re-raised for a new engagement (both the lapse path cur:1059 and the raise path cur:1049 call it unconditionally); TRAIN UP keeps r.166 (mark ends on the hit); TRAIN DOWN keeps r.165 (other marks stay). Write the invariant as a comment beside keepsMark.
2. **One round predicate — roundOpen() IS rangeArmed(); r.193's loosening dropped**
   - *class:* Forked gate predicates: two functions answer 'may a shot happen' and disagree, so a shot is allowed where its lapse is not counted and the light says WAIT
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/tests/drone-playable.test.mjs
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
   - *evidence:* cur:2625 `function roundOpen(){ … if(ph==='PRACTICE'||ph==='LIVE'||!!state.qaArmed) return true; const intro=…; return !!(intro&&intro.style.display==='none'&&ph!=='LIVE'); }` (074ac55c) vs cur:965 `function rangeArmed(){ … return ph==='PRACTICE'||ph==='LIVE'||!!state.qaArmed; }` unchanged · r178:2514 roundOpen and r178:883 rangeArmed are the same predicate · cur:989 `if(!rangeArmed()) return state.rangeMode==='qual40'?'O':'R'` (light RED while roundOpen allows F) · cur:1059 lapse booked only `if(…rangeArmed()&&lapseIsMine()…)` · cur:5186 r.192 `enterTheRange` already sets `state.lobby.phase='PRACTICE'` so r.193 adds nothing to the operator's path, only to WAITING rooms · cur:4600–4601 NO_FIRE_BEFORE_THE_ROUND sets phase WAITING and expects REJECT — green at boot only because the intro is still displayed when the QA runs
   - *why:* r.135's rule (a WAITING room fired from behind the intro — a Thor finding) is re-opened: a tab in a WAITING room with the intro dismissed can mark, approve and fire while the tower shows WAIT and the exposure's lapses are not on the record. The gate that should catch it tests a state the player never meets.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* frontend/tests/drone-playable.test.mjs: regex that `function roundOpen()` returns `rangeArmed()` (one body, or roundOpen delegating) and that no `intro.style.display` read exists inside it; drone-deck-qa row NO_FIRE_BEFORE_THE_ROUND re-done with `intro.style.display='none'` + phase WAITING + qaArmed=false → fireN REJECT row, no round, and designate/approveDesig refused too.
   - *approach:* Delete r.193's body; `function roundOpen(){ return rangeArmed(); }` with the r.135 comment; keep r.192's PRACTICE/host=false on entering The Range and r.187's `?range=&mode=` reader (cur:5224–5225 clamps lane 1..42 and whitelists mode at cur:5175–5176 — safe to carry).
3. **One geometry for the drawn plate, the amber/red box and the hit test (the 'distorted targets' class)**
   - *class:* Split geometry: the painter scales a target the hit test does not, so what the player sees is not what the gate scores

     - frontend/tests/drone-playable.test.mjs
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
     - frontend/tests/range-2525.test.mjs

   - *why:* Operator: 'r.195 is soo beyond messed up as targets dont work', 'distorted targets became a recurring issue'. A 300 m plate at 1× is drawn ~4.5× its hittable size; the amber/red box and the bullseye hold rule stay true-scale, so the picture lies about what can be hit. r.130/r.138/r.141's scale-true rule is the invariant.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* frontend/tests/drone-playable.test.mjs: `silMesh` contains no `<28` scale and plateRect/plateHit/pipOn share plateDims; drone-deck-qa row PLATE_DRAWN_AT_TRUE_SIZE (drawn outline == plateRect ±1 px at 1× and 3×, 50 m and 300 m) and the pits present (RANGE_WIRE segs in the range branch); range-2525 keeps the r.130 scale-true lift.
   - *approach:* r.178's silMesh and RANGE_WIRE draw are the master. If a minimum on-glass size is ever wanted it is applied ONCE in plateDims (the same mrad floor pipFloorPx uses) so painter, box and hit test move together — never in the painter alone.
4. **A red gate never ships — the deck gates run where the deploy actually happens**
   - *class:* Ungated deploy path: the gate exists but the artefact leaves by a door the gate does not stand in
   - *files:* - frontend/package.json
     - .github/workflows/deploy.yml
     - .github/workflows/verify-live.yml
     - frontend/tests/deck-head.mjs
     - frontend/tests/deck-consistency.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* deploy.yml:39 `gate` runs `npm run test:ci` (:76); :201–203 `ship: needs: [gate, backend] if: needs.gate.outputs.canbuild == 'true'` — yet deploy.yml:3–8 records that the Cloudflare git integration deploys on its own, and plan §B shows every main push since d6bcc16 served with gate red · frontend/package.json:9 `prebuild` runs lockcheck/integrity-guard/sign-invariant/financial-surface… but no drone-revisions / deck-consistency / drone-playable / traceability · verify-live.yml:113 reads DECK_REV from deck-head.mjs (:5 `'165'`) and has failed every run since ('does not carry revision 0.165') · drone-playable.test.mjs:18 byte-identity and :22 `revision:'0.165'` are red at HEAD
   - *why:* Every finding in this review reached the operator's phone with the gate red. CLAUDE.md: 'every invariant gets a gate' and 'no outpacing the deploy' — a gate the deploy does not consult is a comment.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* Extend frontend/tests/deck-consistency.test.mjs: `prebuild` in package.json names the four fast deck gates (drone-revisions, deck-consistency, drone-playable, traceability); Verify Live green on the ship SHA before any bookkeeping; a gate (new tests/deploy-door.test.mjs or inside deck-consistency) that DECK_REV == play.html header == BUILD.revision == REVISIONS.md last row.
   - *approach:* Add the four browser-free deck gates to `prebuild` so the Workers build fails red and the site stays on the last green SHA; bump DECK_REV with every served revision (r.196 first); keep deploy.yml's gate as the second door.
5. **The served deck is a carried, hashed, registered revision and the gates read what is served**
   - *class:* Register drift: hand edits to the served artefact leave the carried/hashed/patched chain, so every gate that reads the register proves a dead file
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/REVISIONS.md
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/patches
     - frontend/tests/deck-head.mjs
     - frontend/lib/drone-2525/deck-rev.ts
     - docs/traceability/drone-2525.ledger.json
     - frontend/lib/2525-core/drone-ledger.gen.ts
     - frontend/scripts/gen-rcore-revisions.mjs
   - *evidence:* deck-head.mjs:5–7 `DECK_REV='165'`, `deckUrl` → docs/…/drone-2525_r.165.html; range-2525.test.mjs:13 `fs.readFileSync(deckUrl(…))` — green (118/0) against a file nobody serves · play.html at HEAD: header r0.195 (:349 per plan), BUILD `revision:'0.195'` (cur:730) · r178:652 `BUILD={version:'00.00',revision:'0.166'}` under header r0.178 (r178:318) — decide() stamps `rev:BUILD.revision` on every decision row (cur:2490) and metricsOf (cur:2371) so r.167–r.185 records claim the wrong code · ledger tail revs 69–72 carry `shipped: null` and name the parent commit (72 → 3380e7b while r.195 is 14a930d0); no row for af6f65e0 · r.187–r.189 used twice (7ffd164e)
   - *why:* The record is the product (R-CORE). A decision row stamped 0.166 by a 0.178 deck, a ledger that cites the wrong commit and a gate that reads a frozen snapshot mean nothing shipped since 2026-10-08 can be replayed against the code that made it.
   - *priority:* P1
   - *gate:* frontend/tests/drone-playable.test.mjs (byte identity + `revision:'0.NNN'`), drone-revisions.test.mjs (README/REVISIONS/HASHES chain), deck-consistency.test.mjs (+ header == BUILD == DECK_REV), traceability.test.mjs (no PENDING, commit == artefact sha), and gen-rcore-revisions.mjs gains `--check` so a hand-copied drone-ledger.gen.ts fails.
   - *approach:* Carry drone-2525_r.178.html (af6f65e0 bytes) and r.195 (served bytes) with rows naming them as unregistered hand edits; patcher r195_to_r196.py with asserted rep() anchors; BUILD.revision/header/DECK_REV set together; ledger rev 73 correction + rev 74 release, .gen.ts regenerated never copied.
6. **Wire rows obey the same gate as the buttons — a peer MISS/LAPSE/HIT cannot move my target or my tally without the right on the record**
   - *class:* Trusted wire mutation: rows from another seat change target state and scores with weaker (or no) checks than the local verb
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
     - frontend/tests/deck-qa-manifest.mjs
     - frontend/scripts/drone-team-e2e.mjs
   - *evidence:* cur:2273 `if(row.verb==='MISS' && id && row.peerId && row.peerId!==SID){ const o=findTgt(id); if(o&&o.form){ o._eng=true; …` — no approval check; cur:2594 `if(state.rangeMode==='qual40'&&ref._eng){ … 'ONE ROUND PER TARGET' … REFUSED }` → a peer denies every plate in my QUAL · cur:2274 `if(row.verb==='LAPSE' && id){ … o.up=false; o.fall=1; if(row.peerId!==SID){ … rangeLapse(o,true); state.rangeLapsed++ …` — any sender, not only the lane's clock owner (lapseIsMine cur:966 / hostLane) · cur:2265 HIT: accepted if `(state.events||[]).some(e=>e.verb==='APPROVE'&&e.id===id)` — any APPROVE ever, so in TRAIN UP one approval licenses forged HITs for the rest of the session; the refusal is `log('REJECT',id,'HIT_WITHOUT_RED_BOX')`, not a decide row · r178:2154/2164/2165 identical · commIn cur: `if(m.sessionId && m.sessionId!==state.com.room) return;` — a row with no sessionId crosses rooms; ROW_AUTHOR_MISMATCH (cur:2235-area, r178:1538) rejects only a row signed as me or nobody, not one signed as a third peer
   - *why:* The operator's purpose for ask (4) is a drone swarm where everyone targets and approves before firing. A peer that can down my plates, book my unfired misses or spend my one round per target is the exact thing the two-humans rule exists to prevent. The r.132 comment ('THE WIRE PATH OBEYS THE SAME RULES AS THE BUTTONS') names the invariant; three verbs fall outside it.
   - *priority:* P1
   - *gate:* drone-deck-qa rows beside PEER_APPROVE_OF_A_DEAD_PLATE_REFUSED (cur:4439): PEER_MISS_NEEDS_A_RED_BOX (a peer MISS on an unmarked plate leaves `_eng` false, REJECT row), PEER_LAPSE_ONLY_FROM_THE_CLOCK_OWNER (LAPSE from a non-host peer on my lane → ignored + row), PEER_HIT_STALE_APPROVAL_REFUSED (approve, hit, plate returns, forged HIT → refused); drone-team-e2e two-phone check; drone-link.test.mjs's 'a seat may only send what that seat controls' extended to the range verbs.
   - *approach:* One `wireGate(row)` in applyWorld: HIT/MISS need a red mark for that id in the CURRENT exposure (an APPROVE row after the latest DESIGNATED row for the id and the plate up); LAPSE accepted only from the peer whose clock owns the lane (hostLane); every refusal is `decide('REJECT',id,{reason:'WIRE_…',from})` so the record shows the attempt; require sessionId on every envelope.
7. **Every refusal is a row — TARGET and APPROVE refusals recorded like FIRE's**
   - *class:* Silent refusal: a verb refused with a toast leaves no row, so the record cannot show who tried to mark or approve what
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
     - frontend/tests/drone-decisions.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* cur:2160 `if(!roundOpen()){ toast('PRESS PRACTICE, OR START THE ROOM, BEFORE YOU MARK'); return; }` · cur:2162 `toast('WRONG LANE …'); return;` · cur:2163 `toast('THAT TARGET IS DOWN · MARK ANOTHER'); return;` · cur:2540 `if(!roundOpen()){ toast('… BEFORE YOU APPROVE'); return; }` · cur:2543 `if(!state.desig){toast('NO AMBER · MARK A TARGET FIRST');return;}` — versus fireN cur:2627 `decide('REJECT',…,{reason:'ROUND_NOT_OPEN'})` and cur:2636 `decide('REJECT',…,{reason:'AMBER_NO_APPROVE'|'NO_RED_BOX'})` · r178:2049–2052 and r178:2429 identical
   - *why:* Thor's rule: what is refused is recorded. For a swarm rehearsal the audit question is 'who tried to approve a target that was down / in another lane / before the round' — today those attempts vanish while the same attempt at FIRE is hashed.
   - *priority:* P1
   - *gate:* drone-deck-qa row REFUSALS_ARE_ROWS (a refused TARGET outside the round, on a down plate and on another lane, and a refused APPROVE with no amber, each append one REJECT row with its reason and no state change); drone-decisions.test.mjs for the reason vocabulary.
   - *approach:* A `refuse(verb,id,reason,sentence)` helper = decide('REJECT') + toast, used by designate, approveDesig and fireN; reasons added to the decision vocabulary (MARK_ROUND_NOT_OPEN, MARK_WRONG_LANE, MARK_TARGET_DOWN, APPROVE_NO_AMBER).
8. **TRAIN DOWN START and TIME are decisions behind the gate, on every phone**
   - *class:* Off-record state change: a control that raises targets and starts a clock without a row, outside the round predicate
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
     - frontend/tests/range-2525.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* cur:5314 `_rr.onclick=()=>{ if(chNum()===0&&state.rangeMode==='stay'&&state.downGo!=='run'&&state.downGo!=='done'){ downStart(); toast('START · HIT EVERY TARGET IN THE LANE'); … return; } if(rangeResetOnRecord('BUTTON')) …` — START is the only branch that skips rangeResetOnRecord/decide · cur:954 downStart resets rangeHit/rangeMiss and raises plates locally · cur:1044 the clock stops locally with a toast, no row · cur:5309 rangeResetOnRecord shows the pattern START must follow · r178 has no timer (r178:915 qualTimer empty outside QUAL)
   - *why:* The time under the light is the number the operator wants to compare across runs (and the seed of the 3v3 challenge). A time that is not a row cannot be verified, replayed or compared; in a room two phones start two clocks and tally two scores.
   - *priority:* P1
   - *gate:* drone-deck-qa rows TRAIN_DOWN_ON_THE_RECORD (START and TIME rows present and hashed; START refused outside the round with a REJECT row) and TRAIN_DOWN_CLOCK (the TIME row equals the clock ±50 ms); range-2525 lifts downArm/downStart.
   - *approach:* Port aac4db9b onto r.178 with START → `decide('START',lane,{mode:'stay'})` and the last plate down → `decide('TIME',lane,{ms})`, both through ev()/linkSend like RESET; START refused when !rangeArmed().

     - frontend/lib/wire-core/vector-law.ts

11. **The magazine card is sized from the drawing — the 48:96 drawing never changes proportion**
   - *class:* Layout by hand-tuned constants: card sizes re-typed every revision (r.183–r.191) instead of derived from the art, so each edit 'distorts' the magazines
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
   - *evidence:* r178:29–37 `#magWell{left:8px;top:92px;gap:12px}` cards `width:86px;min-height:112px`, svg `36px×82px`; cur:38 svg `24px×50px`; neither sets preserveAspectRatio (only the QR svg does, cur:5928 / r178:5793) so the art letterboxes rather than stretches — the 'distortion' the operator sees is the card and row moving under it (04d76634, 60d5a411, 12962de7, 6a4097f6, a146c120, 5a6b64bb) · cur:4700-area layout QA row rewritten in r.195 to match the moved row
   - *why:* Operator: 'ensure magazines don't get distorted' (shots 10–11 are the master). A row re-measured by hand each revision is a row that will move again.
   - *priority:* P2
   - *gate:* drone-deck-qa row MAG_ROW_ONE_LINE (RELOAD, count and light share one `top`; count centred ±2 px; magazine svg rendered width/height = 0.5 ± 0.02; card height == light height) replacing r.195's rewritten layout row.
   - *approach:* Set the svg box to the drawing's ratio (28×56 for 48:96), let the card take its size from it (padding + label), the light the same height, and never type a card size again.
