**r178_screenshot_match:** No — af6f65e0's CSS does not produce shots 1–2. Code: r178:29 `#magWell{position:absolute;left:8px;top:92px;…;gap:12px}`, r178:31–33 both cards `width:86px … min-height:112px` side by side (RELOAD, then the count 12 px to its right), r178:34 svg `36×82`, r178:50 `#startLight{position:absolute;right:8px;top:42px}` — the light 50 px ABOVE the cards' top; grep of af6f65e0 finds no JS that writes magWell/startLight style. Picture (shot1, 1284×2778 at 3× = 428 CSS px): RELOAD card at x≈8–60, the count card centred (x≈195–234, centre ≈214 = 428/2), the light at right 8 with its top level with the cards' top (y≈213 CSS), cards ≈56×68 CSS px, header 'eXeL r0.178', status 'LAST · 150 M LEFT · DOWN', 'HIT · 150 M LEFT · TARGET · APPROVE · FIRE'. The text and colours are af6f65e0's; the row is not. Nearest committed layouts: a146c120 (r0.189, count centred) and 6a4097f6 (r0.186, compact cards). Either the live build at 04:40 differed from the git bytes (deploy.yml:3–8 documents hand `wrangler deploy`s) or a later hand edit was served under the r0.178 header. The headless 428×926 render of af6f65e0 in step 0 should still be kept beside shot 1 as the proof, but the direction is settled: the PICTURE is the visual master, af6f65e0 the code base.

**minor_notes:** - commIn (cur ~2230) drops only rows signed as me or nobody (ROW_AUTHOR_MISMATCH); an embedded row signed as a third peer is accepted — fine in two-seat rooms, a gap for the 3v3 challenge
- commIn: `if(m.sessionId && m.sessionId!==state.com.room) return;` — an envelope without sessionId crosses rooms on the same BroadcastChannel ('exel-2525-com', cur:1380); require sessionId
- LOBBY_LAUNCH from any peer runs lobbyEnterLive (cur ~2258) and importSnapshotDesig sets lobby.phase='LIVE' from a snapshot — a joiner can flip the host LIVE; by design today, worth a host check later
- mark() (dock FIRE / Space, cur:2679) calls fireN when a desig exists, so each tap on an amber box writes a REJECT AMBER_NO_APPROVE row — record noise, not an exploit; consider a HOLD without a row for repeat taps within 1 s
- The r.148 peer-HIT refusal is deliberately `log()` not `decide()` so an honest peer never forks the hash (cur:2265) — keep that reasoning but mark the local row `committed:false` so the attempt is visible in replay
- The `?range=` reader (cur:5224–5225) clamps the lane to 1..42 and enterTheRange whitelists the mode (cur:5175–5176); `role=` is ignored — safe; the QR text at r.178 pointed at play.html?range= which nothing read
- RCORE_HIST is rendered with innerHTML from a literal (cur:724) — no injection today; a ledger-fed port must use textContent since ledger text is operator-authored
- range-2525.test.mjs:91 asserts the r.142 'PRESS RELOAD · MOVE' phase toast; cur:1061 says 'CEASE FIRE · LOCK AND LOAD' — the gate is green only because it reads the carried r.165 file
- Verify Live cannot be reached from the sandbox (proxy 403); UNVERIFIED must be said until the operator's phone or the Actions log confirms each SHA
- Sensor Fusion (app/SensorFusion-2525, its own ledger.ts) is untouched by every fix above and is credited to the Grok bot

**summary:** Thor's finding: the range has three fire gates where it must have one. (1) A QUAL approval outlives its exposure — modeReturns/keepsMark/rangeRelease keep a red mark through a lapse or hit and IWQ_VI re-raises the same plates, so from phase 2 on a target can be fired with no new TARGET/APPROVE, at r0.178 and r0.195 alike, and the deck's own QA row (qualKept) certifies it; restore r.169 and rewrite the rows (P0). (2) r.193 forked roundOpen() from rangeArmed(): a WAITING room with the intro dismissed fires while the light says WAIT and lapses are not booked, and the boot QA row stays green only because the intro is up when it runs; roundOpen must BE rangeArmed (P0). (3) r.180's silMesh draws small plates up to 4.5× their hittable rect in every mode while plateRect/plateHit/pipOn stay true — the picture invites a shot the gate refuses, the operator's 'targets don't work'; r.178's one geometry is the master (P0). (4) All of it shipped with the deck gates red because the Cloudflare build never consults them and DECK_REV is frozen at 165 — put the four fast deck gates in prebuild and move DECK_REV with every served revision (P0). Then the wire: peer MISS/LAPSE/HIT rows change my targets and tallies with weaker checks than the buttons (P1); TARGET/APPROVE refusals are toasts, not rows (P1); TRAIN DOWN START/TIME are off the record and outside the gate (P1); the fixed R-CORE badge sits on the dock's own offset and opens a full-screen sheet while the clock runs, with a hand-typed history off the shared vocabulary (P1). Keep af6f65e0 as the code base (its gate, geometry, pits, refusal ladder, AI rules, magazine art); keep from after it only the TRAIN DOWN clock (as rows), the cold-range end, r.192's PRACTICE entry + the `?range=` reader, the pose selector (art replaced), and r.189's centred-count CSS as the picture's model; drop r.193, r.180's enlargement, r.195's badge/history, the filled poses and every QA row edited to match a defect. The operator's screenshots are not af6f65e0's CSS (cards 86 px side by side, light 50 px above) — the picture is the visual master; a headless render of af6f65e0 at 428×926 is the proof to keep beside it.

### Thoth (data & analytics deep dive)

**verdict_on_current:** Through the record lens, r0.195 is an unregistered artefact and r0.178 is an unrecorded one. The ledger docs/traceability/drone-2525.ledger.json is genuinely append-only (a commit-by-commit replay of the 12 Grok ledger commits shows only appends, no row changed or removed) but every row it appended is wrong in a systematic way: revs 64–72 each cite the sha of the commit that added the PREVIOUS row (rev 64 r.187→61b4d49, 65 r.188→7ffd164 … 72 r.195→3380e7b; the real r.195 is 14a930d0) because the row was written inside the commit it describes; rev 61 (r.176) has commit PENDING and was reverted four minutes later by b2dfa80a, filed as a RELEASE (rev 62) that cites the original d6bcc160, not the revert; rev 63 is a release filed as a decision; revs 61–72 carry no `shipped` key at all, which deck-consistency.test.mjs:29 only inspects when the key exists; rev 60 shipped PENDING, domain JSON 0.050 shipped PENDING and the register's `shipped in` column reads "PENDING (Verify Live)" on all 15 rows r.151–r.165 — the ship field has been a dead placeholder since r.150. No row exists for r.167–r.175 or r.177–r.185, so the operator's baseline r0.178 (af6f65e0) has no sha on the record. Inside play.html the revision number lives twice — `<span>r0.NNN</span>` (r178:318, cur:349) and `BUILD.revision` (r178:652 = '0.166', cur:730) — and they disagree in 20 of the 35 hand-edit commits (r0.177–r0.186 all shipped BUILD '0.166'; r0.187–r0.189 were used twice, corner 0.189 reset to 0.187 by 7ffd164e); Verify Live greps BUILD (verify-live.yml:113) while the operator reads the corner. The deck register stopped at r.165 (deck-head.mjs:5, deck-rev.ts:3, HASHES_r165, patches/r164_to_r165.py, README HEAD) while 41 commits with zero Co-Authored-By trailers edited play.html by hand; range-2525.test.mjs:10-13 reads the CARRIED r.165 file so its green proves a dead file; drone-ledger.gen.ts was hand-copied (538 vs 519 generated lines, 20 `shipped` keys the generator at gen-rcore-revisions.mjs:58-64 never emits). In the game, the decision record has the same five decide() kinds at r178 and cur (APPROVE, HOLD, REJECT, RELOAD, RESET); TRAIN DOWN's START (cur:5314, downStart() without decide) and the ALL DOWN stop (cur:1042, toast only) are off the record, `state.downT` is read only by downClockWord (cur:952), and the QUAL end is an ev('QUAL',…,'DONE h/r') string (cur:971), not a decision. The deck also carries three private histories — #updBox UPDATES text (r178:550 16 lines; cur 25 lines), RCORE_HIST (cur:709-715, kind 'approved', rev strings) and the corner — none fed by the ledger the React route reads (command-ux1.tsx:147).

**fixes:**

1. **A release row must cite the commit that carries it — never the parent, never PENDING forever**
   - *class:* ledger row written inside the commit it describes (sha unknowable) with no gate that the cited commit actually carries the revision
   - *files:* - docs/traceability/drone-2525.ledger.json
     - frontend/tests/traceability.test.mjs
     - frontend/tests/deck-consistency.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* Commit-by-commit replay of the ledger: 7ffd164e added rev 64 (r.187) commit=61b4d49; 8206a28b added rev 65 (r.188) commit=7ffd164; a146c120 rev 66 (r.189) commit=8206a28; 3ba0f97c rev 67 commit=a146c12; 5a6b64bb rev 68 commit=3ba0f97; 43a0dc46 rev 69 commit=5a6b64b; 074ac55c rev 70 commit=43a0dc4; 3380e7b1 rev 71 commit=074ac55; 14a930d0 rev 72 (r.195) commit=3380e7b — each cites the commit that added the previous row. 82b3c0bb added rev 61 commit=PENDING (still PENDING at HEAD). traceability.test.mjs:27 only checks `exists(e.commit)`; nothing checks that `git show <commit>:frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html` carries revision:'0.NNN'. The house pattern (revs 56–60: commit = artefact sha, shipped = bookkeeping sha, e.g. rev 59 commit bf8cb7c shipped a5764e1; a5764e15 'r.164 records: artefact commit after sync is bf8cb7c') was abandoned at rev 61.
   - *why:* The R-CORE panel's commit chips, the compare tool and any audit of 'what was served when' resolve to the wrong bytes for nine rows; rev 61 keeps the traceability gate red (CI dies at that step and hides every later gate), and the record cannot name the commit for the operator's own baseline.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* Extend frontend/tests/traceability.test.mjs (Drone section): for every release row, `git show <commit>:frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html` must contain revision:'0.NNN' where NNN is the r.NNN named in the row's text (cheap cat-file, no checkout); PENDING allowed on the last release only. Red-first: fails on revs 61 and 64–72 today.
   - *approach:* Never edit 64–72. Fill rev 61 `commit` = 82b3c0bb (the designed placeholder fill). Append rev 73 kind `correction` that lists the true artefact sha per row (r.187 7ffd164e · r.188 8206a28b · r.189 a146c120 · r.190 3ba0f97c · r.191 5a6b64bb · r.192 43a0dc46 · r.193 074ac55c · r.194 3380e7b1 · r.195 14a930d0), states r.176 was served ~4 min then reverted by b2dfa80a, and restores the two-commit pattern (artefact commit → bookkeeping commit after Verify Live) for r.196 onward.
2. **One revision number in the file: the corner reads BUILD.revision, no literal r0.NNN in markup**
   - *class:* two sources of the same identity in one artefact with no gate holding them equal
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/tests/drone-playable.test.mjs
     - .github/workflows/verify-live.yml
   - *evidence:* r178:318 `<b>eXeL</b><span>r0.178</span>` vs r178:652 `const BUILD={version:'00.00',revision:'0.166'}`; cur:349 `<span>r0.195</span>` vs cur:730 revision:'0.195'. Per-commit table: corner/BUILD disagree at bb7bb3cd (167/0.166), 7cbe041c (175/0.174), 82b3c0bb (176/0.174), dc495fe6→6a4097f6 (177–186 / 0.166, ten commits incl. af6f65e0), ddb5108d/040a21ba/61b4d49d (187/188/189 / 0.186); 7ffd164e then re-used 187, 8206a28b 188, a146c120 189. verify-live.yml:113-115 greps `revision:'0.$REV'` (BUILD); drone-playable.test.mjs:22 same; the operator's screenshots show the corner.
   - *why:* The operator's 'r0.178' is BUILD '0.166' — the one string every gate and Verify Live reads cannot identify what he saw; three numbers name two byte-sets each; any ledger/register row keyed on the corner is unverifiable.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* drone-playable.test.mjs: `<span id="rev">` is empty in markup and set from BUILD.revision at boot (regex: no literal /r0\.\d{3}</span>/ in the file; `set('rev','r'+BUILD.revision)` present); verify-live.yml greps BUILD and the register sha as today. Red-first on r0.178 and r0.195 bytes.
   - *approach:* In the r.196 patcher: replace the literal corner with an id and one line in boot that writes 'r'+BUILD.revision; the patcher asserts both anchors (a miss refuses). The correction row records which corner numbers were served with which BUILD values.
3. **The ship field is a dead placeholder — make `shipped` mandatory and filled by the bookkeeping commit**
   - *class:* a placeholder nobody is obliged to fill, plus a gate that skips rows lacking the key
   - *files:* - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/REVISIONS.md
     - docs/traceability/drone-2525.ledger.json
     - docs/drone-2525/drone-2525.v00.00.json
     - frontend/tests/deck-consistency.test.mjs
     - frontend/tests/drone-revisions.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* REVISIONS.md rows r.151–r.165 all read `PENDING (Verify Live)` in the `shipped in` column (15 rows; r.165 row at REVISIONS.md:48). Ledger rev 60 `shipped: PENDING`; domain JSON 0.050 `shipped: PENDING, commit 47f66e2`; ledger releases without any `shipped` key: revs 61,62,64–72 (and 4–11, 24–40 historically). deck-consistency.test.mjs:28-29 `if (e.shipped !== undefined || i === last)` — a release with NO shipped key is never checked. drone-revisions.test.mjs:13-14 parses `shipped` and asserts nothing about it; the chain (line 25) is sha256(prev|rev|sha|bytes) so `shipped` is outside the chain and can be filled safely. The r.165 ship was 4bb4a3f7 (2026-10-05) and its bookkeeping commit never came — the next ledger commit is Grok's 82b3c0bb.
   - *why:* 'Never outpace the deploy' (CLAUDE.md:4-11) is enforced by the bookkeeping commit; with the field optional and the gate skipping absent keys, nine releases shipped with no ship sha and nobody can say which commit put which bytes on the edge.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* deck-consistency.test.mjs: EVERY release row (ledger and domain JSON) must carry `shipped` — a 7–40 hex sha, or PENDING only on the last; drone-revisions.test.mjs: the register's `shipped in` column is a sha for every row but the last. Red-first: fails on rev 60/0.050 and the 15 PENDING register rows today.
   - *approach:* Bookkeeping commit after Verify Live green on r.196 fills rev 60 (the commit that first served r.165 bytes), 0.050 and the register column r.151–r.165 (chain-safe); every later release gets its `shipped` from the same bookkeeping step, never from the artefact commit.
4. **The register is the only door to play.html, and a red deck gate must fail the Cloudflare build**
   - *class:* served artefact edited outside its carried/hashed/patched/registered chain while the deploy door ignores the gate
   - *files:* - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/REVISIONS.md
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/HASHES_r196.sha256
     - docs/drone-2525/operator-deck/patches/r195_to_r196.py
     - frontend/tests/deck-head.mjs
     - frontend/lib/drone-2525/deck-rev.ts
     - frontend/package.json
     - frontend/tests/range-2525.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* git log play.html: 35 hand-edit commits d6bcc160→14a930d0 (41 commits in the span, 0 Co-Authored-By trailers; d6bcc160 tz -0500, the rest +0000) with no carried file (last is drone-2525_r.165.html), no HASHES after HASHES_r165.sha256, no patch after r164_to_r165.py, no register row after r.165 (REVISIONS.md:48), deck-head.mjs:5 DECK_REV='165', deck-rev.ts:3 '165'. Served play.html 547,290 B sha 7b02aed7…; af6f65e0 531,981 B sha d39875be… — neither in any register. range-2525.test.mjs:10-13 reads deckUrl() = the carried r.165 file, so 118/0 green proves a dead file. package.json `prebuild` runs lockcheck/integrity/seal/sign/sf/financial gates only — none of drone-playable, deck-consistency, drone-revisions, traceability; deploy.yml:3-6 records hand `wrangler deploy` as a known path.
   - *why:* The operator's baseline has no sha on the record; Verify Live has been red on every run since 2026-10-08 ('does not carry revision 0.165') yet production serves r0.195; a fix that is not carried, hashed and patched cannot be replayed, compared or reverted by the record.
   - *priority:* P0
   - *gate:* deck-consistency + drone-playable + drone-revisions + traceability added to `prebuild` (fast, no browser) so Cloudflare Workers Builds fails on a red register; drone-playable byte-identity re-pointed to r.196; range-2525 additionally asserts the served public/drone-2525/play.html equals the carried HEAD (not only the carried file).
   - *approach:* Carry drone-2525_r.178.html (= af6f65e0 bytes) and drone-2525_r.195.html (served bytes) as register rows with author 'hand edit via the operator's account (Grok), unregistered at the time' and the 35 shas listed in the r.196 section; r.196 built by patches/r195_to_r196.py whose SRC is the r.178 file; HASHES_r196 (deck + patch); DECK_REV/deck-rev '196'; domain JSON 0.051; ledger rev 74 release with commit = artefact sha, shipped PENDING until Verify Live.
5. **Reversals, re-numberings and false claims are `correction` rows, never releases or decisions**
   - *class:* a change of the record filed under the wrong kind so the ledger says two contradictory things with nothing between them
   - *files:* - docs/traceability/drone-2525.ledger.json
     - frontend/tests/traceability.test.mjs
   - *evidence:* rev 62 (b2dfa80a 'Reinstate the r.166 range', −2,450 lines restoring d6bcc160 bytes) kind=release, commit=d6bcc160 (the original, not the revert); rev 63 'LOCKED GLASS r0.186 served' kind=decision (a release); rev 66 'The round count is the center card' vs rev 68 'The round count is the next card, not in the center' — no correction between them; rev 72 'R-CORE now matches Vision-2525' while cur:68 `#rcoreBadge{position:fixed…z-index:9}` and cur:709-715 RCORE_HIST has 5 hand rows with kind 'approved' (rcore-revisions.test.mjs:107 forbids a fixed badge; KINDS at traceability.test.mjs:11 are ask|decision|release|correction). The vocabulary exists and was used correctly four times (revs 13, 18, 23, 25: 'CORRECTING REVISION 0.006 ON THE RECORD…', 'Register row r.128 cited d195781 as the shipping commit; r.128 was carried and served by 2159671…').
   - *why:* The shared panel ranks kinds (revisions.ts:129-132: correction L4 > release L3 > decision L2); a revert filed as a release reads as progress, and the compare tool counts rev 62 and 63 as 'added' instead of 'revised' (revisions.ts:228-230).
   - *priority:* P1
   - *gate:* traceability.test.mjs (Drone section): the r.NNN named in a release row's text must be strictly greater than every earlier release's r.NNN (fails on rev 62 r.166 after rev 61 r.176 and rev 64 r.187 after corner 0.189) unless the row is kind `correction`; each correction row must name the rev it corrects ('rev N' or 'r.NNN').
   - *approach:* Rev 73 (correction) states: rev 62 was a revert of r.167–r.176; rev 63 was a release of r0.186 (6a4097f6); r.187–r.189 name two byte-sets each (ddb5108d/040a21ba/61b4d49d, then 7ffd164e/8206a28b/a146c120); rev 66 vs 68 — the operator's picture (count centred) is the master, DD-01; rev 72's claim was false (fixed badge, hand list) and is answered by r.199.
6. **Generated modules are reproducible by the generator — add `--check` and gate all .gen.ts**
   - *class:* a generated artefact edited by hand with no gate that regeneration reproduces it
   - *files:* - frontend/lib/2525-core/drone-ledger.gen.ts

     - frontend/tests/rcore-revisions.test.mjs

The script has no `--check` flag and is in no test:ci step.
   - *why:* The React route's R-CORE history (command-ux1.tsx:147 ← DRONE_LEDGER) and the deck's future embedded RCORE_LEDGER must be the same bytes as the ledger file; a hand copy drifts silently and the type `shipped?` added at revisions.ts:58 'to unblock the build' papered over it.

   - *approach:* Decide once whether `shipped` is part of the typed snapshot (then the generator emits it and the panel may show it) or not (then drop `shipped?` from LedgerEntryInput); re-run the generator; never hand-edit a .gen.ts.
7. **Every scored transition is a decide() row: TRAIN DOWN START and TIME, QUAL COLD**
   - *class:* state transitions the operator will score kept in display state only, not in the decision record
   - *files:* - frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html
     - frontend/tests/range-2525.test.mjs
     - frontend/tests/deck-qa-manifest.mjs
     - frontend/scripts/drone-deck-qa.mjs
   - *evidence:* cur:5314 `_rr.onclick=()=>{ if(…state.rangeMode==='stay'&&state.downGo!=='run'…){ downStart(); toast('START · …'); … return; } if(rangeResetOnRecord('BUTTON')) … }` — START never reaches decide(); cur:5312 RESTART does: `decide('RESET','RANGE',{why,mode:state.rangeMode,targets,lane,qualR,qualH})`. cur:1042 `if(state.rangeAllDown){ state.downGo='done'; toast('ALL DOWN · '+downClockWord()); }` — stop is a toast. `downT` consumers: cur:952 downClockWord, :953 downArm, :954 downStart, :1042 — never in decide/ev/sample/RANGE_RUN/localStorage. cur:971 qualFinish writes `ev('QUAL','P'+ph,'DONE '+h+'/'+r)` only when lapseIsMine() — an event with the score inside a string, no decision. decide kinds identical at r178 and cur: APPROVE×3, HOLD×15, REJECT×14, RELOAD×1, RESET×1. decide():2494 forwards only HOLD/REJECT/RESET/RELOAD over the link, so START/TIME would not reach the other phone even as events; ev() rows (cur:2338-2342) carry no lane or mode field.
   - *why:* Operator addendum 8: TRAIN DOWN's time is the seed of the 3v3 best-time challenge — a time that is not a hashed, replayable row cannot be compared across runs or rooms; the QUAL score likewise.
   - *priority:* P1
   - *gate:* range-2525.test.mjs: source regexes for decide('START','RANGE',{mode:'stay',lane,targets}), decide('TIME','RANGE',{ms,…}) and decide('COLD','RANGE',{hits,rounds,score}); deck QA rows TRAIN_DOWN_ON_THE_RECORD (START and TIME rows in state.decisions, TIME.ms within ±50 ms of state.downT, present in replayHash()) and QUAL_ENDS_COLD_ON_THE_RECORD; ids added to deck-qa-manifest.mjs.
   - *approach:* In r.197/r.198: route START, the last-target-down stop and qualFinish through decide() (which calls ev() and linkSend) with structured data (mode, lane, ms, hits); the status line reads the recorded value, not state.downT.

   - *class:* re-serialisation noise hiding content changes in an append-only file

Review by diff could not see that only rev 61 was added; the in-memory replay had to prove it.

**r178_screenshot_match:** Partly, and the record cannot settle it. The corner `<span>r0.178</span>` exists in exactly ONE commit — af6f65e0 (r178:318); every other hand edit shows a different corner (per-commit table: dc495fe6 r0.177, 50826908 r0.179 … 6a4097f6 r0.186; 60d5a411/12962de7 say r0.184/185, not 178), so the corner, the 'LAST · …' status line and the round colours in shots 1–2 identify af6f65e0's HTML and JS. But af6f65e0's BUILD.revision is '0.166' (r178:652), its CSS (r178:29-37/50, per explorer C) lays the two 86×112 cards side by side at left 8 / top 92 with the light above at top 42, whereas the shots show RELOAD left, the count card CENTRED and the light on the SAME row — nearest committed layouts are a146c120 (r0.189) and 6a4097f6 (r0.186). From the data side there is no way to check what was served on 2026-10-10/11: r.178 has no ledger row, no register row and no hash; Verify Live was red on every run; deploy.yml:3-6 documents hand `wrangler deploy` as a known path, so the live bytes need not equal any commit. I did not render (read-only run). Verdict: af6f65e0 is provably the SOURCE of the picture's code, not provably its layout — adopt the picture as the visual master by decision (DD-01) and keep the 428×926 headless render of af6f65e0 beside shot 1 in the r.196 notes as the evidence.

**minor_notes:** - asks-persist is intact: the sidecar's hash ce17a7332d0d… equals sha256 of the ask .md and the sidecar lists shots 1–11
- deck-consistency.test.mjs:28-29 loophole: `if (r.shipped !== undefined || last)` — a release row with no `shipped` key is never checked; this is why revs 61–71 do not fail today while rev 60's PENDING does
- the register chain (drone-revisions.test.mjs:25) is sha256(prev|rev|sha|bytes) — `shipped in` is outside the chain, so filling the 15 PENDING cells is chain-safe; the narrative 'Artefact commit' fill (a5764e15) is the same designed placeholder pattern
- revisions.ts:44 says 'PENDING is kept verbatim, so the panel can say so' while :62 says a PENDING commit 'should not read as a shipped commit' and commitOf() passes PENDING through — one of the two comments is wrong; the shared panel shows a PENDING chip for rev 61 today
- ev() rows (cur:2338-2342) carry challenge/diff/role/verb/id/result/data but no lane or range mode; START/TIME/COLD rows must put lane and mode in `data` (as RESET does at cur:5312)
- QA row ids did not drift (207 static push ids at both r178 and cur; the manifest's 213 include dynamic ids) — the drift is semantic (rows edited in place), which no manifest can catch; the red-first run on old bytes is the only gate for that
- I could not find the 'r.195 rewritten layout order row' (`on[0].left<on[2].left<on[1].left`) in cur or r178 with a push(...left<...) search — explorer C's claim is unverified by me
- traceability's rev-61 failure is the step where CI dies (traceability is step 27 of test:ci; drone-playable/range/deck-consistency/deck-qa come later), so the three red deck gates have been hidden behind one PENDING string since 2026-10-10
- Sensor Fusion (app/SensorFusion-2525, its own ledger.ts) is untouched by every finding here and is credited to the Grok bot in the correction row — nothing in this lens proposes an edit there

**summary:** Thoth: the Drone-2525 record is append-only in form but wrong in content and empty where it matters. Four P0 classes: (1) ledger release rows written inside the artefact commit cite the parent sha (revs 64–72) or PENDING forever (rev 61) — gate that the cited commit carries the row's revision, correct by a rev-73 correction row and the two-commit pattern; (2) two revision numbers in one file (corner r0.NNN vs BUILD.revision) disagree in 20 of 35 hand edits — one source, gated; (3) the ship field is a dead placeholder (register PENDING r.151–r.165, ledger rev 60 and domain 0.050 PENDING, revs 61–72 keyless and skipped by the gate) — make `shipped` mandatory and filled by the bookkeeping commit; (4) 35 unregistered hand edits serve production while the deck gates are absent from prebuild — carry r.178 and r.195, build r.196 from r.178 by patch, put the register gates in the Cloudflare door. P1: reversals/re-numberings/false claims filed as releases (revs 62, 63, 66↔68, 72; r.187–189 used twice) become correction rows with a monotonic-number gate; the hand-copied drone-ledger.gen.ts gets a regenerate-and-compare gate; TRAIN DOWN START/TIME and QUAL COLD become decide() rows (today START bypasses decide at play.html:5314, the stop is a toast at :1042, downT is display-only); the deck's three private histories (RCORE_HIST, #updBox, corner) are replaced by the embedded shared ledger. P2: canonical ledger serialisation, provenance (41 trailer-less commits), titles that say what changed with DD-NN decisions. Keep af6f65e0 as the code base and its decision record; keep the r.182 clock, the r.179/181 cold range, the r.169 QUAL fix and the pose-card logic on top; drop the enlarge, the roundOpen loosening, the fixed badge and hand lists. The screenshots identify af6f65e0's code (its corner string is unique to that commit) but not its CSS, and the record holds no hash for r.178 — adopt the picture by decision and render af6f65e0 at step 0.
